<a id="appendix.application-properties"></a>

# Common Application Properties

Various properties can be specified inside your `application.properties` file, inside your `application.yaml` file, or as command line switches.
This appendix provides a list of common Spring Boot properties and references to the underlying classes that consume them.

> [!TIP]
> Spring Boot provides various conversion mechanisms with advanced value formatting.
> Make sure to review [the properties conversion section](../../reference/features/external-config.md#features.external-config.typesafe-configuration-properties.conversion).

> [!NOTE]
> Property contributions can come from additional jar files on your classpath, so you should not consider this an exhaustive list.
> Also, you can define your own properties.

If more measurements are found, then multiple requests will be made. | `10000` |

The longest match wins, the key `all` can also be used to configure all meters. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.maximum-expected-value"></a>[`management.metrics.distribution.maximum-expected-value.*`](#application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.maximum-expected-value) | Maximum value that meter IDs starting with the specified name are expected to observe. The longest match wins. Values can be specified as a double or, for timer and long-task timer meters, as a Duration value defaulting to ms if no unit specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.minimum-expected-value"></a>[`management.metrics.distribution.minimum-expected-value.*`](#application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.minimum-expected-value) | Minimum value that meter IDs starting with the specified name are expected to observe. The longest match wins. Values can be specified as a double or, for timer and long-task timer meters, as a Duration value defaulting to ms if no unit specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.percentiles"></a>[`management.metrics.distribution.percentiles.*`](#application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.percentiles) | Specific computed non-aggregable percentiles to ship to the backend for meter IDs starting-with the specified name. The longest match wins, the key 'all' can also be used to configure all meters. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.percentiles-histogram"></a>[`management.metrics.distribution.percentiles-histogram.*`](#application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.percentiles-histogram) | Whether meter IDs starting with the specified name should publish percentile histograms. For monitoring systems that support aggregable percentile calculation based on a histogram, this can be set to true. For other systems, this has no effect. The longest match wins, the key 'all' can also be used to configure all meters. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.slo"></a>[`management.metrics.distribution.slo.*`](#application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.distribution.slo) | Specific service-level objective boundaries for meter IDs starting with the specified name. The longest match wins. Counters will be published for each specified boundary. Values can be specified as a double or, for timer and long-task timer meters, as a Duration value defaulting to ms if no unit specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.enable"></a>[`management.metrics.enable.*`](#application-properties.actuator.management.metrics.enable) | Whether meter IDs starting with the specified name should be enabled. | <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.observations.r2dbc.include-parameter-values"></a>[`management.observations.r2dbc.include-parameter-values`](#application-properties.actuator.management.observations.r2dbc.include-parameter-values) | Whether to tag actual query parameter values. | `false` |

If OpenTelemetry is disabled, only propagators are configured. Metrics, traces, and logging will use no-op implementations. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.opentelemetry.logging.export.max-batch-size"></a>[`management.opentelemetry.logging.export.max-batch-size`](#application-properties.actuator.management.opentelemetry.logging.export.max-batch-size) | Maximum batch size for each export. This must be less than or equal to 'maxQueueSize'. | `512` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.opentelemetry.logging.export.max-queue-size"></a>[`management.opentelemetry.logging.export.max-queue-size`](#application-properties.actuator.management.opentelemetry.logging.export.max-queue-size) | Maximum number of logs that are kept in the queue before they will be dropped. | `2048` |

If the call requires redirects or retries all must complete within one timeout period. | `10s` |

| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.server.ssl.trust-store-password"></a>[`management.server.ssl.trust-store-password`](#application-properties.actuator.management.server.ssl.trust-store-password) | Password used to access the trust store. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.server.ssl.trust-store-type"></a>[`management.server.ssl.trust-store-type`](#application-properties.actuator.management.server.ssl.trust-store-type) | Type of the trust store. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.server.tomcat.accesslog.prefix"></a>[`management.server.tomcat.accesslog.prefix`](#application-properties.actuator.management.server.tomcat.accesslog.prefix) | Management log file name prefix. | `management_` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.simple.metrics.export.enabled"></a>[`management.simple.metrics.export.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.simple.metrics.export.enabled) | Whether exporting of metrics to this backend is enabled. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.simple.metrics.export.mode"></a>[`management.simple.metrics.export.mode`](#application-properties.actuator.management.simple.metrics.export.mode) | Counting mode. | `cumulative` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.simple.metrics.export.step"></a>[`management.simple.metrics.export.step`](#application-properties.actuator.management.simple.metrics.export.step) | Step size (i.e. reporting frequency) to use. | `1m` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.auto-create-metric-descriptors"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.auto-create-metric-descriptors`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.auto-create-metric-descriptors) | Whether it should be attempted to create a metric descriptor before writing a time series. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.batch-size"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.batch-size`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.batch-size) | Number of measurements per request to use for this backend. If more measurements are found, then multiple requests will be made. | `10000` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.connect-timeout"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.connect-timeout`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.connect-timeout) | Connection timeout for requests to this backend. | `1s` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.enabled"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.enabled) | Whether exporting of metrics to this backend is enabled. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.metric-type-prefix"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.metric-type-prefix`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.metric-type-prefix) | Prefix for metric type. Valid prefixes are described in the Google Cloud documentation (https://cloud.google.com/monitoring/custom-metrics#identifier). | `custom.googleapis.com/` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.project-id"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.project-id`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.project-id) | Identifier of the Google Cloud project to monitor. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.read-timeout"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.read-timeout`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.read-timeout) | Read timeout for requests to this backend. | `10s` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.resource-labels"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.resource-labels.*`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.resource-labels) | Monitored resource's labels. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.resource-type"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.resource-type`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.resource-type) | Monitored resource type. | `global` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.step"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.step`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.step) | Step size (i.e. reporting frequency) to use. | `1m` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.use-semantic-metric-types"></a>[`management.stackdriver.metrics.export.use-semantic-metric-types`](#application-properties.actuator.management.stackdriver.metrics.export.use-semantic-metric-types) | Whether to use semantically correct metric types. When false, counter metrics are published as the GAUGE MetricKind. When true, counter metrics are published as the CUMULATIVE MetricKind. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.buffered"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.buffered`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.buffered) | Whether measurements should be buffered before sending to the StatsD server. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.enabled"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.enabled) | Whether exporting of metrics to StatsD is enabled. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.flavor"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.flavor`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.flavor) | StatsD line protocol to use. | `datadog` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.host"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.host`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.host) | Host of the StatsD server to receive exported metrics. | `localhost` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.max-packet-length"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.max-packet-length`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.max-packet-length) | Total length of a single payload should be kept within your network's MTU. | `1400` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.polling-frequency"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.polling-frequency`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.polling-frequency) | How often gauges will be polled. When a gauge is polled, its value is recalculated and if the value has changed (or publishUnchangedMeters is true), it is sent to the StatsD server. | `10s` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.port"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.port`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.port) | Port of the StatsD server to receive exported metrics. | `8125` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.protocol"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.protocol`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.protocol) | Protocol of the StatsD server to receive exported metrics. | `udp` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.publish-unchanged-meters"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.publish-unchanged-meters`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.publish-unchanged-meters) | Whether to send unchanged meters to the StatsD server. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.step"></a>[`management.statsd.metrics.export.step`](#application-properties.actuator.management.statsd.metrics.export.step) | Step size to use in computing windowed statistics like max. To get the most out of these statistics, align the step interval to be close to your scrape interval. | `1m` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.correlation.enabled"></a>[`management.tracing.baggage.correlation.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.correlation.enabled) | Whether to enable correlation of the baggage context with logging contexts. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.correlation.fields"></a>[`management.tracing.baggage.correlation.fields`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.correlation.fields) | List of fields that should be correlated with the logging context. That means that these fields would end up as key-value pairs in e.g. MDC. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.enabled"></a>[`management.tracing.baggage.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.enabled) | Whether to enable Micrometer Tracing baggage propagation. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.local-fields"></a>[`management.tracing.baggage.local-fields`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.local-fields) | List of fields that should be accessible within the JVM process but not propagated over the wire. Local fields are not supported with OpenTelemetry. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.remote-fields"></a>[`management.tracing.baggage.remote-fields`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.remote-fields) | List of fields that are referenced the same in-process as it is on the wire. For example, the field "x-vcap-request-id" would be set as-is including the prefix. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.tag-fields"></a>[`management.tracing.baggage.tag-fields`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.baggage.tag-fields) | List of fields that should automatically become tags. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.exemplars.include"></a>[`management.tracing.exemplars.include`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.exemplars.include) | Traces to include as exemplars. 'all' is not supported when using Prometheus. | `sampled-traces` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.enabled"></a>[`management.tracing.export.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.enabled) | Whether auto-configuration of tracing is enabled to export and propagate traces. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.otlp.enabled"></a>[`management.tracing.export.otlp.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.otlp.enabled) | Whether auto-configuration of tracing is enabled to export OTLP traces. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.connect-timeout"></a>[`management.tracing.export.zipkin.connect-timeout`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.connect-timeout) | Connection timeout for requests to Zipkin. | `1s` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.enabled"></a>[`management.tracing.export.zipkin.enabled`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.enabled) | Whether auto-configuration of tracing is enabled to export Zipkin traces. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.encoding"></a>[`management.tracing.export.zipkin.encoding`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.encoding) | How to encode the POST body to the Zipkin API. | `json` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.endpoint"></a>[`management.tracing.export.zipkin.endpoint`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.endpoint) | URL to the Zipkin API. | `http://localhost:9411/api/v2/spans` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.read-timeout"></a>[`management.tracing.export.zipkin.read-timeout`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.export.zipkin.read-timeout) | Read timeout for requests to Zipkin. | `10s` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.propagation.consume"></a>[`management.tracing.propagation.consume`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.propagation.consume) | Tracing context propagation types consumed by the application. | `[W3C, B3, B3_MULTI]` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.propagation.produce"></a>[`management.tracing.propagation.produce`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.propagation.produce) | Tracing context propagation types produced by the application. | `[W3C]` |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.propagation.type"></a>[`management.tracing.propagation.type`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.propagation.type) | Tracing context propagation types produced and consumed by the application. Setting this property overrides the more fine-grained propagation type properties. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.sampling.probability"></a>[`management.tracing.sampling.probability`](#application-properties.actuator.management.tracing.sampling.probability) | Probability in the range from 0.0 to 1.0 that a trace will be sampled. | `0.1` |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.cache"></a>

## Cache Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.cache-names"></a>[`spring.cache.cache-names`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.cache-names) | List of cache names to create if supported by the underlying cache manager. Usually, this disables the ability to create additional caches on-the-fly. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.caffeine.spec"></a>[`spring.cache.caffeine.spec`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.caffeine.spec) | The spec to use to create caches. See CaffeineSpec for more details on the spec format. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.couchbase.expiration"></a>[`spring.cache.couchbase.expiration`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.couchbase.expiration) | Entry expiration. By default the entries never expire. Note that this value is ultimately converted to seconds. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.infinispan.config"></a>[`spring.cache.infinispan.config`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.infinispan.config) | The location of the configuration file to use to initialize Infinispan. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.jcache.config"></a>[`spring.cache.jcache.config`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.jcache.config) | The location of the configuration file to use to initialize the cache manager. The configuration file is dependent of the underlying cache implementation. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.jcache.provider"></a>[`spring.cache.jcache.provider`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.jcache.provider) | Fully qualified name of the CachingProvider implementation to use to retrieve the JSR-107 compliant cache manager. Needed only if more than one JSR-107 implementation is available on the classpath. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.cache-null-values"></a>[`spring.cache.redis.cache-null-values`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.cache-null-values) | Allow caching null values. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.enable-statistics"></a>[`spring.cache.redis.enable-statistics`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.enable-statistics) | Whether to enable cache statistics. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.key-prefix"></a>[`spring.cache.redis.key-prefix`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.key-prefix) | Key prefix. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.time-to-live"></a>[`spring.cache.redis.time-to-live`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.time-to-live) | Entry expiration. By default the entries never expire. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.use-key-prefix"></a>[`spring.cache.redis.use-key-prefix`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.redis.use-key-prefix) | Whether to use the key prefix when writing to Redis. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.cache.spring.cache.type"></a>[`spring.cache.type`](#application-properties.cache.spring.cache.type) | Cache type. By default, auto-detected according to the environment. |  |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.core"></a>

## Core Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.core.debug"></a>[`debug`](#application-properties.core.debug) | Enable debug logs. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.info"></a>[`info.*`](#application-properties.core.info) | Arbitrary properties to add to the info endpoint. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.charset.console"></a>[`logging.charset.console`](#application-properties.core.logging.charset.console) | Charset to use for console output. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.charset.file"></a>[`logging.charset.file`](#application-properties.core.logging.charset.file) | Charset to use for file output. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.config"></a>[`logging.config`](#application-properties.core.logging.config) | Location of the logging configuration file. For instance, `classpath:logback.xml` for Logback. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.console.enabled"></a>[`logging.console.enabled`](#application-properties.core.logging.console.enabled) | Whether to enable console-based logging. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.exception-conversion-word"></a>[`logging.exception-conversion-word`](#application-properties.core.logging.exception-conversion-word) | Conversion word used when logging exceptions. | `%wEx` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.file.name"></a>[`logging.file.name`](#application-properties.core.logging.file.name) | Log file name (for instance, `myapp.log`). Names can be an exact location or relative to the current directory. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.file.path"></a>[`logging.file.path`](#application-properties.core.logging.file.path) | Location of the log file. For instance, `/var/log`. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.group"></a>[`logging.group.*`](#application-properties.core.logging.group) | Log groups to quickly change multiple loggers at the same time. For instance, `logging.group.db=org.hibernate,org.springframework.jdbc`. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.include-application-group"></a>[`logging.include-application-group`](#application-properties.core.logging.include-application-group) | Whether to include the application group in the logs. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.include-application-name"></a>[`logging.include-application-name`](#application-properties.core.logging.include-application-name) | Whether to include the application name in the logs. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.level"></a>[`logging.level.*`](#application-properties.core.logging.level) | Log levels severity mapping. For instance, `logging.level.org.springframework=DEBUG`. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.config.override"></a>[`logging.log4j2.config.override`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.config.override) | Overriding configuration files used to create a composite configuration. Can be prefixed with 'optional:' to only load the override if it exists. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.cron"></a>[`logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.cron`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.cron) | Cron expression used when the strategy is 'cron'. | `0 0 0 * * ?` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.file-name-pattern"></a>[`logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.file-name-pattern`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.file-name-pattern) | Pattern for rolled-over log file names. | `${LOG_FILE}.%d{yyyy-MM-dd}.%i.gz` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.max-file-size"></a>[`logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.max-file-size`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.max-file-size) | Maximum log file size. | `10MB` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.max-history"></a>[`logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.max-history`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.max-history) | Maximum number of archive log files to keep. | `7` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.strategy"></a>[`logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.strategy`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.strategy) | Rolling policy strategy. | `size` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.time-interval"></a>[`logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.time-interval`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.time-interval) | Time based triggering interval when the strategy is 'time' or 'size-and-time'. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.time-modulate"></a>[`logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.time-modulate`](#application-properties.core.logging.log4j2.rollingpolicy.time-modulate) | Whether to align the next rollover time to occur at the top of the interval when the strategy is time based. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.clean-history-on-start"></a>[`logging.logback.rollingpolicy.clean-history-on-start`](#application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.clean-history-on-start) | Whether to clean the archive log files on startup. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.file-name-pattern"></a>[`logging.logback.rollingpolicy.file-name-pattern`](#application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.file-name-pattern) | Pattern for rolled-over log file names. | `${LOG_FILE}.%d{yyyy-MM-dd}.%i.gz` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.max-file-size"></a>[`logging.logback.rollingpolicy.max-file-size`](#application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.max-file-size) | Maximum log file size. | `10MB` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.max-history"></a>[`logging.logback.rollingpolicy.max-history`](#application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.max-history) | Maximum number of archive log files to keep. | `7` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.total-size-cap"></a>[`logging.logback.rollingpolicy.total-size-cap`](#application-properties.core.logging.logback.rollingpolicy.total-size-cap) | Total size of log backups to be kept. | `0B` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.pattern.console"></a>[`logging.pattern.console`](#application-properties.core.logging.pattern.console) | Appender pattern for output to the console. Its default value varies according to the logging system. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.pattern.correlation"></a>[`logging.pattern.correlation`](#application-properties.core.logging.pattern.correlation) | Appender pattern for log correlation. Its default value varies according to the logging system. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.pattern.dateformat"></a>[`logging.pattern.dateformat`](#application-properties.core.logging.pattern.dateformat) | Appender pattern for log date format. | `yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.SSSXXX` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.pattern.file"></a>[`logging.pattern.file`](#application-properties.core.logging.pattern.file) | Appender pattern for output to a file. Its default value varies according to the logging system. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.pattern.level"></a>[`logging.pattern.level`](#application-properties.core.logging.pattern.level) | Appender pattern for log level. | `%5p` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.register-shutdown-hook"></a>[`logging.register-shutdown-hook`](#application-properties.core.logging.register-shutdown-hook) | Register a shutdown hook for the logging system when it is initialized. Disabled automatically when deployed as a war file. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.environment"></a>[`logging.structured.ecs.service.environment`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.environment) | Structured ECS service environment. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.name"></a>[`logging.structured.ecs.service.name`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.name) | Structured ECS service name (defaults to 'spring.application.name'). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.node-name"></a>[`logging.structured.ecs.service.node-name`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.node-name) | Structured ECS service node name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.version"></a>[`logging.structured.ecs.service.version`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.ecs.service.version) | Structured ECS service version (defaults to 'spring.application.version'). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.format.console"></a>[`logging.structured.format.console`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.format.console) | Structured logging format for output to the console. Must be either a format id or a fully qualified class name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.format.file"></a>[`logging.structured.format.file`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.format.file) | Structured logging format for output to a file. Must be either a format id or a fully qualified class name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.gelf.host"></a>[`logging.structured.gelf.host`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.gelf.host) | Structured GELF host (defaults to 'spring.application.name'). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.gelf.service.version"></a>[`logging.structured.gelf.service.version`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.gelf.service.version) | Structured GELF service version (defaults to 'spring.application.version'). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.add"></a>[`logging.structured.json.add.*`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.add) | Additional members that should be added to structured logging JSON |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.context.include"></a>[`logging.structured.json.context.include`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.context.include) | Whether context data should be included in the JSON. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.context.prefix"></a>[`logging.structured.json.context.prefix`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.context.prefix) | The prefix to use when inserting context data. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.customizer"></a>[`logging.structured.json.customizer`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.customizer) | Fully qualified class names of StructuredLoggingJsonMembersCustomizer implementations |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.exclude"></a>[`logging.structured.json.exclude`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.exclude) | Member paths that should be excluded from structured logging JSON |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.include"></a>[`logging.structured.json.include`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.include) | Member paths that should be included in structured logging JSON |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.rename"></a>[`logging.structured.json.rename.*`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.rename) | Mapping between member paths and an alternative name that should be used in structured logging JSON |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.include-common-frames"></a>[`logging.structured.json.stacktrace.include-common-frames`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.include-common-frames) | Whether common frames should be included. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.include-hashes"></a>[`logging.structured.json.stacktrace.include-hashes`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.include-hashes) | Whether stack trace hashes should be included. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.max-length"></a>[`logging.structured.json.stacktrace.max-length`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.max-length) | Maximum length to print. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.max-throwable-depth"></a>[`logging.structured.json.stacktrace.max-throwable-depth`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.max-throwable-depth) | Maximum throwable depth to print. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.printer"></a>[`logging.structured.json.stacktrace.printer`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.printer) | Name of the printer to use. Can be 'standard', 'logging-system', or the fully-qualified class name of a StackTracePrinter. When not specified 'logging-system' or 'standard' will be used depending if other properties are set. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.root"></a>[`logging.structured.json.stacktrace.root`](#application-properties.core.logging.structured.json.stacktrace.root) | Root ordering (root first or root last). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.threshold.console"></a>[`logging.threshold.console`](#application-properties.core.logging.threshold.console) | Log level threshold for console output. | `TRACE` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.logging.threshold.file"></a>[`logging.threshold.file`](#application-properties.core.logging.threshold.file) | Log level threshold for file output. | `TRACE` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.aop.auto"></a>[`spring.aop.auto`](#application-properties.core.spring.aop.auto) | Add @EnableAspectJAutoProxy. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.aop.proxy-target-class"></a>[`spring.aop.proxy-target-class`](#application-properties.core.spring.aop.proxy-target-class) | Whether subclass-based (CGLIB) proxies are to be created (true), as opposed to standard Java interface-based proxies (false). | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.application.admin.enabled"></a>[`spring.application.admin.enabled`](#application-properties.core.spring.application.admin.enabled) | Whether to enable admin features for the application. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.application.group"></a>[`spring.application.group`](#application-properties.core.spring.application.group) | Application group. Typically used with logging to help identify the application. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.application.name"></a>[`spring.application.name`](#application-properties.core.spring.application.name) | Application name. Typically used with logging to help identify the application. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.config.name"></a>[`spring.config.name`](#application-properties.core.spring.config.name) | Config file name. | `application` |

| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.jmx.default-domain"></a>[`spring.jmx.default-domain`](#application-properties.core.spring.jmx.default-domain) | JMX domain name. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.lifecycle.timeout-per-shutdown-phase"></a>[`spring.lifecycle.timeout-per-shutdown-phase`](#application-properties.core.spring.lifecycle.timeout-per-shutdown-phase) | Timeout for the shutdown of any phase (group of SmartLifecycle beans with the same 'phase' value). | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.allow-bean-definition-overriding"></a>[`spring.main.allow-bean-definition-overriding`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.allow-bean-definition-overriding) | Whether bean definition overriding, by registering a definition with the same name as an existing definition, is allowed. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.allow-circular-references"></a>[`spring.main.allow-circular-references`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.allow-circular-references) | Whether to allow circular references between beans and automatically try to resolve them. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.banner-mode"></a>[`spring.main.banner-mode`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.banner-mode) | Mode used to display the banner when the application runs. Defaults to 'off' if structured logging is enabled or to 'console' otherwise |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.cloud-platform"></a>[`spring.main.cloud-platform`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.cloud-platform) | Override the Cloud Platform auto-detection. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.keep-alive"></a>[`spring.main.keep-alive`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.keep-alive) | Whether to keep the application alive even if there are no more non-daemon threads. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.lazy-initialization"></a>[`spring.main.lazy-initialization`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.lazy-initialization) | Whether initialization should be performed lazily. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.log-startup-info"></a>[`spring.main.log-startup-info`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.log-startup-info) | Whether to log information about the application when it starts. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.register-shutdown-hook"></a>[`spring.main.register-shutdown-hook`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.register-shutdown-hook) | Whether the application should have a shutdown hook registered. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.sources"></a>[`spring.main.sources`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.sources) | Sources (class names, package names, or XML resource locations) to include in the ApplicationContext. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.main.web-application-type"></a>[`spring.main.web-application-type`](#application-properties.core.spring.main.web-application-type) | Flag to explicitly request a specific type of web application. If not set, auto-detected based on the classpath. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.mandatory-file-encoding"></a>[`spring.mandatory-file-encoding`](#application-properties.core.spring.mandatory-file-encoding) | Expected character encoding the application must use. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.messages.always-use-message-format"></a>[`spring.messages.always-use-message-format`](#application-properties.core.spring.messages.always-use-message-format) | Whether to always apply the MessageFormat rules, parsing even messages without arguments. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.messages.basename"></a>[`spring.messages.basename`](#application-properties.core.spring.messages.basename) | List of basenames (essentially a fully-qualified classpath location), each following the ResourceBundle convention with relaxed support for slash based locations. If it doesn't contain a package qualifier (such as "org.mypackage"), it will be resolved from the classpath root. | `[messages]` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.messages.cache-duration"></a>[`spring.messages.cache-duration`](#application-properties.core.spring.messages.cache-duration) | Loaded resource bundle files cache duration. When not set, bundles are cached forever. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.messages.common-messages"></a>[`spring.messages.common-messages`](#application-properties.core.spring.messages.common-messages) | List of locale-independent property file resources containing common messages. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.messages.encoding"></a>[`spring.messages.encoding`](#application-properties.core.spring.messages.encoding) | Message bundles encoding. | `UTF-8` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.messages.fallback-to-system-locale"></a>[`spring.messages.fallback-to-system-locale`](#application-properties.core.spring.messages.fallback-to-system-locale) | Whether to fall back to the system Locale if no files for a specific Locale have been found. if this is turned off, the only fallback will be the default file (e.g. "messages.properties" for basename "messages"). | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.messages.use-code-as-default-message"></a>[`spring.messages.use-code-as-default-message`](#application-properties.core.spring.messages.use-code-as-default-message) | Whether to use the message code as the default message instead of throwing a "NoSuchMessageException". Recommended during development only. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.output.ansi.enabled"></a>[`spring.output.ansi.enabled`](#application-properties.core.spring.output.ansi.enabled) | Configures the ANSI output. | `detect` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.pid.fail-on-write-error"></a>[`spring.pid.fail-on-write-error`](#application-properties.core.spring.pid.fail-on-write-error) | Fails if ApplicationPidFileWriter is used but it cannot write the PID file. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.pid.file"></a>[`spring.pid.file`](#application-properties.core.spring.pid.file) | Location of the PID file to write (if ApplicationPidFileWriter is used). |  |

| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.profiles.default"></a>[`spring.profiles.default`](#application-properties.core.spring.profiles.default) | Name of the profile to enable if no profile is active. | `default` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.profiles.group"></a>[`spring.profiles.group.*`](#application-properties.core.spring.profiles.group) | Profile groups to define a logical name for a related group of profiles. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.profiles.include"></a>[`spring.profiles.include`](#application-properties.core.spring.profiles.include) | Unconditionally activate the specified comma-separated list of profiles (or list of profiles if using YAML). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.profiles.validate"></a>[`spring.profiles.validate`](#application-properties.core.spring.profiles.validate) | Whether profiles should be validated to ensure sensible names are used. | `true` |
