| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.comment-prefix"></a>[`spring.quartz.jdbc.comment-prefix`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.comment-prefix) | Prefixes for single-line comments in SQL initialization scripts. | `[#, --]` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.continue-on-error"></a>[`spring.quartz.jdbc.continue-on-error`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.continue-on-error) | Whether initialization should continue when an error occurs when applying a schema script. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.initialize-schema"></a>[`spring.quartz.jdbc.initialize-schema`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.initialize-schema) | Database schema initialization mode. | `embedded` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.platform"></a>[`spring.quartz.jdbc.platform`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.platform) | Platform to use in initialization scripts if the @@platform@@ placeholder is used. Auto-detected by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.schema"></a>[`spring.quartz.jdbc.schema`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.jdbc.schema) | Path to the SQL file to use to initialize the database schema. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.job-store-type"></a>[`spring.quartz.job-store-type`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.job-store-type) | Quartz job store type. | `memory` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.overwrite-existing-jobs"></a>[`spring.quartz.overwrite-existing-jobs`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.overwrite-existing-jobs) | Whether configured jobs should overwrite existing job definitions. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.properties"></a>[`spring.quartz.properties.*`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.properties) | Additional Quartz Scheduler properties. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.scheduler-name"></a>[`spring.quartz.scheduler-name`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.scheduler-name) | Name of the scheduler. | `quartzScheduler` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.startup-delay"></a>[`spring.quartz.startup-delay`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.startup-delay) | Delay after which the scheduler is started once initialization completes. Setting this property makes sense if no jobs should be run before the entire application has started up. | `0s` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.quartz.wait-for-jobs-to-complete-on-shutdown"></a>[`spring.quartz.wait-for-jobs-to-complete-on-shutdown`](#application-properties.core.spring.quartz.wait-for-jobs-to-complete-on-shutdown) | Whether to wait for running jobs to complete on shutdown. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.reactor.context-propagation"></a>[`spring.reactor.context-propagation`](#application-properties.core.spring.reactor.context-propagation) | Context Propagation support mode for Reactor operators. | `limited` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.reactor.debug-agent.enabled"></a>[`spring.reactor.debug-agent.enabled`](#application-properties.core.spring.reactor.debug-agent.enabled) | Whether the Reactor Debug Agent should be enabled when reactor-tools is present. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.reactor.netty.shutdown-quiet-period"></a>[`spring.reactor.netty.shutdown-quiet-period`](#application-properties.core.spring.reactor.netty.shutdown-quiet-period) | Amount of time to wait before shutting down resources. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.ssl.bundle.jks"></a>[`spring.ssl.bundle.jks.*`](#application-properties.core.spring.ssl.bundle.jks) | Java keystore SSL trust material. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.ssl.bundle.pem"></a>[`spring.ssl.bundle.pem.*`](#application-properties.core.spring.ssl.bundle.pem) | PEM-encoded SSL trust material. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.ssl.bundle.watch.file.quiet-period"></a>[`spring.ssl.bundle.watch.file.quiet-period`](#application-properties.core.spring.ssl.bundle.watch.file.quiet-period) | Quiet period, after which changes are detected. | `10s` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.mode"></a>[`spring.task.execution.mode`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.mode) | Determine when the task executor is to be created. | `auto` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.allow-core-thread-timeout"></a>[`spring.task.execution.pool.allow-core-thread-timeout`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.allow-core-thread-timeout) | Whether core threads are allowed to time out. This enables dynamic growing and shrinking of the pool. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.core-size"></a>[`spring.task.execution.pool.core-size`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.core-size) | Core number of threads. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. | `8` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.keep-alive"></a>[`spring.task.execution.pool.keep-alive`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.keep-alive) | Time limit for which threads may remain idle before being terminated. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. | `60s` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.max-size"></a>[`spring.task.execution.pool.max-size`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.max-size) | Maximum allowed number of threads. If tasks are filling up the queue, the pool can expand up to that size to accommodate the load. Ignored if the queue is unbounded. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.queue-capacity"></a>[`spring.task.execution.pool.queue-capacity`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.queue-capacity) | Queue capacity. An unbounded capacity does not increase the pool and therefore ignores the "max-size" property. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.shutdown.accept-tasks-after-context-close"></a>[`spring.task.execution.pool.shutdown.accept-tasks-after-context-close`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.pool.shutdown.accept-tasks-after-context-close) | Whether to accept further tasks after the application context close phase has begun. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.propagate-context"></a>[`spring.task.execution.propagate-context`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.propagate-context) | Whether to propagate the current context to task executions. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.shutdown.await-termination"></a>[`spring.task.execution.shutdown.await-termination`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.shutdown.await-termination) | Whether the executor should wait for scheduled tasks to complete on shutdown. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.shutdown.await-termination-period"></a>[`spring.task.execution.shutdown.await-termination-period`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.shutdown.await-termination-period) | Maximum time the executor should wait for remaining tasks to complete. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.simple.cancel-remaining-tasks-on-close"></a>[`spring.task.execution.simple.cancel-remaining-tasks-on-close`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.simple.cancel-remaining-tasks-on-close) | Whether to cancel remaining tasks on close. Only recommended if threads are commonly expected to be stuck. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.simple.concurrency-limit"></a>[`spring.task.execution.simple.concurrency-limit`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.simple.concurrency-limit) | Set the maximum number of parallel accesses allowed. -1 indicates no concurrency limit at all. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.simple.reject-tasks-when-limit-reached"></a>[`spring.task.execution.simple.reject-tasks-when-limit-reached`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.simple.reject-tasks-when-limit-reached) | Whether to reject tasks when the concurrency limit has been reached. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.thread-name-prefix"></a>[`spring.task.execution.thread-name-prefix`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.execution.thread-name-prefix) | Prefix to use for the names of newly created threads. | `task-` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.pool.size"></a>[`spring.task.scheduling.pool.size`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.pool.size) | Maximum allowed number of threads. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.shutdown.await-termination"></a>[`spring.task.scheduling.shutdown.await-termination`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.shutdown.await-termination) | Whether the executor should wait for scheduled tasks to complete on shutdown. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.shutdown.await-termination-period"></a>[`spring.task.scheduling.shutdown.await-termination-period`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.shutdown.await-termination-period) | Maximum time the executor should wait for remaining tasks to complete. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.simple.concurrency-limit"></a>[`spring.task.scheduling.simple.concurrency-limit`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.simple.concurrency-limit) | Set the maximum number of parallel accesses allowed. -1 indicates no concurrency limit at all. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.thread-name-prefix"></a>[`spring.task.scheduling.thread-name-prefix`](#application-properties.core.spring.task.scheduling.thread-name-prefix) | Prefix to use for the names of newly created threads. | `scheduling-` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.threads.virtual.enabled"></a>[`spring.threads.virtual.enabled`](#application-properties.core.spring.threads.virtual.enabled) | Whether to use virtual threads. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.spring.validation.method.adapt-constraint-violations"></a>[`spring.validation.method.adapt-constraint-violations`](#application-properties.core.spring.validation.method.adapt-constraint-violations) | Whether to adapt ConstraintViolations to MethodValidationResult. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.core.trace"></a>[`trace`](#application-properties.core.trace) | Enable trace logs. | `false` |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.data-migration"></a>

## Data Migration Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.baseline-description"></a>[`spring.flyway.baseline-description`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.baseline-description) | Description to tag an existing schema with when applying a baseline. | `<< Flyway Baseline >>` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.baseline-on-migrate"></a>[`spring.flyway.baseline-on-migrate`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.baseline-on-migrate) | Whether to automatically call baseline when migrating a non-empty schema. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.baseline-version"></a>[`spring.flyway.baseline-version`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.baseline-version) | Version to tag an existing schema with when executing baseline. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.batch"></a>[`spring.flyway.batch`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.batch) | Whether to batch SQL statements when executing them. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.callback-locations"></a>[`spring.flyway.callback-locations`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.callback-locations) | Locations of callbacks. Can contain the special "{vendor}" placeholder to use vendor-specific callbacks. Unprefixed locations or locations starting with "classpath:" point to a package on the classpath and may contain both SQL and Java-based callbacks. Locations starting with "filesystem:" point to a directory on the filesystem, may only contain SQL callbacks. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.clean-disabled"></a>[`spring.flyway.clean-disabled`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.clean-disabled) | Whether to disable cleaning of the database. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.community-db-support-enabled"></a>[`spring.flyway.community-db-support-enabled`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.community-db-support-enabled) | Whether to enable community database support. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.connect-retries"></a>[`spring.flyway.connect-retries`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.connect-retries) | Maximum number of retries when attempting to connect to the database. | `0` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.connect-retries-interval"></a>[`spring.flyway.connect-retries-interval`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.connect-retries-interval) | Maximum time between retries when attempting to connect to the database. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `120s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.create-schemas"></a>[`spring.flyway.create-schemas`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.create-schemas) | Whether Flyway should attempt to create the schemas specified in the schemas property. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.default-schema"></a>[`spring.flyway.default-schema`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.default-schema) | Default schema name managed by Flyway (case-sensitive). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.detect-encoding"></a>[`spring.flyway.detect-encoding`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.detect-encoding) | Whether to attempt to automatically detect SQL migration file encoding. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.driver-class-name"></a>[`spring.flyway.driver-class-name`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.driver-class-name) | Fully qualified name of the JDBC driver. Auto-detected based on the URL by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.dry-run-output"></a>[`spring.flyway.dry-run-output`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.dry-run-output) | File to which the SQL statements of a migration dry run should be output. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.enabled"></a>[`spring.flyway.enabled`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.enabled) | Whether to enable flyway. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.encoding"></a>[`spring.flyway.encoding`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.encoding) | Encoding of SQL migrations. | `UTF-8` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.error-overrides"></a>[`spring.flyway.error-overrides`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.error-overrides) | Rules for the built-in error handling to override specific SQL states and error codes. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.execute-in-transaction"></a>[`spring.flyway.execute-in-transaction`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.execute-in-transaction) | Whether Flyway should execute SQL within a transaction. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.fail-on-missing-locations"></a>[`spring.flyway.fail-on-missing-locations`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.fail-on-missing-locations) | Whether to fail if a location of migration scripts doesn't exist. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.group"></a>[`spring.flyway.group`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.group) | Whether to group all pending migrations together in the same transaction when applying them. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.ignore-migration-patterns"></a>[`spring.flyway.ignore-migration-patterns`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.ignore-migration-patterns) | List of patterns that identify migrations to ignore when performing validation. | `*:future` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.init-sqls"></a>[`spring.flyway.init-sqls`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.init-sqls) | SQL statements to execute to initialize a connection immediately after obtaining it. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.installed-by"></a>[`spring.flyway.installed-by`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.installed-by) | Username recorded in the schema history table as having applied the migration. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.jdbc-properties"></a>[`spring.flyway.jdbc-properties.*`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.jdbc-properties) | Properties to pass to the JDBC driver. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.kerberos-config-file"></a>[`spring.flyway.kerberos-config-file`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.kerberos-config-file) | Path of the Kerberos config file. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.locations"></a>[`spring.flyway.locations`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.locations) | Locations of migrations scripts. Can contain the special "{vendor}" placeholder to use vendor-specific locations. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.lock-retry-count"></a>[`spring.flyway.lock-retry-count`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.lock-retry-count) | Maximum number of retries when trying to obtain a lock. | `50` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.loggers"></a>[`spring.flyway.loggers`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.loggers) | Loggers Flyway should use. | `[slf4j]` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.mixed"></a>[`spring.flyway.mixed`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.mixed) | Whether to allow mixing transactional and non-transactional statements within the same migration. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.kerberos-cache-file"></a>[`spring.flyway.oracle.kerberos-cache-file`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.kerberos-cache-file) | Path of the Oracle Kerberos cache file. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.sqlplus"></a>[`spring.flyway.oracle.sqlplus`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.sqlplus) | Whether to enable support for Oracle SQL\*Plus commands. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.sqlplus-warn"></a>[`spring.flyway.oracle.sqlplus-warn`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.sqlplus-warn) | Whether to issue a warning rather than an error when a not-yet-supported Oracle SQL\*Plus statement is encountered. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.wallet-location"></a>[`spring.flyway.oracle.wallet-location`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.oracle.wallet-location) | Location of the Oracle Wallet, used to sign in to the database automatically. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.out-of-order"></a>[`spring.flyway.out-of-order`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.out-of-order) | Whether to allow migrations to be run out of order. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.output-query-results"></a>[`spring.flyway.output-query-results`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.output-query-results) | Whether Flyway should output a table with the results of queries when executing migrations. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.password"></a>[`spring.flyway.password`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.password) | Login password of the database to migrate. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-prefix"></a>[`spring.flyway.placeholder-prefix`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-prefix) | Prefix of placeholders in migration scripts. | `${` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-replacement"></a>[`spring.flyway.placeholder-replacement`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-replacement) | Perform placeholder replacement in migration scripts. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-separator"></a>[`spring.flyway.placeholder-separator`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-separator) | Separator of default placeholders. | `:` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-suffix"></a>[`spring.flyway.placeholder-suffix`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholder-suffix) | Suffix of placeholders in migration scripts. | `}` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholders"></a>[`spring.flyway.placeholders.*`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.placeholders) | Placeholders and their replacements to apply to sql migration scripts. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.postgresql.transactional-lock"></a>[`spring.flyway.postgresql.transactional-lock`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.postgresql.transactional-lock) | Whether transactional advisory locks should be used. If set to false, session-level locks are used instead. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.powershell-executable"></a>[`spring.flyway.powershell-executable`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.powershell-executable) | PowerShell executable to use for running PowerShell scripts. Default to "powershell" on Windows, "pwsh" on other platforms. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.repeatable-sql-migration-prefix"></a>[`spring.flyway.repeatable-sql-migration-prefix`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.repeatable-sql-migration-prefix) | File name prefix for repeatable SQL migrations. | `R` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.schemas"></a>[`spring.flyway.schemas`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.schemas) | Scheme names managed by Flyway (case-sensitive). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.script-placeholder-prefix"></a>[`spring.flyway.script-placeholder-prefix`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.script-placeholder-prefix) | Prefix of placeholders in migration scripts. | `FP__` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.script-placeholder-suffix"></a>[`spring.flyway.script-placeholder-suffix`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.script-placeholder-suffix) | Suffix of placeholders in migration scripts. | `__` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.skip-default-callbacks"></a>[`spring.flyway.skip-default-callbacks`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.skip-default-callbacks) | Whether to skip default callbacks. If true, only custom callbacks are used. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.skip-default-resolvers"></a>[`spring.flyway.skip-default-resolvers`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.skip-default-resolvers) | Whether to skip default resolvers. If true, only custom resolvers are used. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.skip-executing-migrations"></a>[`spring.flyway.skip-executing-migrations`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.skip-executing-migrations) | Whether Flyway should skip executing the contents of the migrations and only update the schema history table. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sql-migration-prefix"></a>[`spring.flyway.sql-migration-prefix`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sql-migration-prefix) | File name prefix for SQL migrations. | `V` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sql-migration-separator"></a>[`spring.flyway.sql-migration-separator`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sql-migration-separator) | File name separator for SQL migrations. | `__` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sql-migration-suffixes"></a>[`spring.flyway.sql-migration-suffixes`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sql-migration-suffixes) | File name suffix for SQL migrations. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sqlserver.kerberos-login-file"></a>[`spring.flyway.sqlserver.kerberos-login-file`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.sqlserver.kerberos-login-file) | Path to the SQL Server Kerberos login file. Requires Flyway Teams. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.stream"></a>[`spring.flyway.stream`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.stream) | Whether to stream SQL migrations when executing them. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.table"></a>[`spring.flyway.table`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.table) | Name of the schema history table that will be used by Flyway. | `flyway_schema_history` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.tablespace"></a>[`spring.flyway.tablespace`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.tablespace) | Tablespace in which the schema history table is created. Ignored when using a database that does not support tablespaces. Defaults to the default tablespace of the connection used by Flyway. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.target"></a>[`spring.flyway.target`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.target) | Target version up to which migrations should be considered. | `latest` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.url"></a>[`spring.flyway.url`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.url) | JDBC url of the database to migrate. If not set, the primary configured data source is used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.user"></a>[`spring.flyway.user`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.user) | Login user of the database to migrate. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.validate-migration-naming"></a>[`spring.flyway.validate-migration-naming`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.validate-migration-naming) | Whether to validate migrations and callbacks whose scripts do not obey the correct naming convention. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.validate-on-migrate"></a>[`spring.flyway.validate-on-migrate`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.flyway.validate-on-migrate) | Whether to automatically call validate when performing a migration. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.analytics-enabled"></a>[`spring.liquibase.analytics-enabled`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.analytics-enabled) | Whether to send product usage data and analytics to Liquibase. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.change-log"></a>[`spring.liquibase.change-log`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.change-log) | Change log configuration path. | `classpath:/db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.clear-checksums"></a>[`spring.liquibase.clear-checksums`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.clear-checksums) | Whether to clear all checksums in the current changelog, so they will be recalculated upon the next update. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.contexts"></a>[`spring.liquibase.contexts`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.contexts) | List of runtime contexts to use. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.database-change-log-lock-table"></a>[`spring.liquibase.database-change-log-lock-table`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.database-change-log-lock-table) | Name of table to use for tracking concurrent Liquibase usage. | `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.database-change-log-table"></a>[`spring.liquibase.database-change-log-table`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.database-change-log-table) | Name of table to use for tracking change history. | `DATABASECHANGELOG` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.default-schema"></a>[`spring.liquibase.default-schema`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.default-schema) | Default database schema. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.driver-class-name"></a>[`spring.liquibase.driver-class-name`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.driver-class-name) | Fully qualified name of the JDBC driver. Auto-detected based on the URL by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.drop-first"></a>[`spring.liquibase.drop-first`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.drop-first) | Whether to first drop the database schema. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.enabled"></a>[`spring.liquibase.enabled`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.enabled) | Whether to enable Liquibase support. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.label-filter"></a>[`spring.liquibase.label-filter`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.label-filter) | List of runtime labels to use. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.license-key"></a>[`spring.liquibase.license-key`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.license-key) | Liquibase Pro license key. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.liquibase-schema"></a>[`spring.liquibase.liquibase-schema`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.liquibase-schema) | Schema to use for Liquibase objects. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.liquibase-tablespace"></a>[`spring.liquibase.liquibase-tablespace`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.liquibase-tablespace) | Tablespace to use for Liquibase objects. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.parameters"></a>[`spring.liquibase.parameters.*`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.parameters) | Change log parameters. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.password"></a>[`spring.liquibase.password`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.password) | Login password of the database to migrate. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.rollback-file"></a>[`spring.liquibase.rollback-file`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.rollback-file) | File to which rollback SQL is written when an update is performed. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.show-summary"></a>[`spring.liquibase.show-summary`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.show-summary) | Whether to print a summary of the update operation. | `summary` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.show-summary-output"></a>[`spring.liquibase.show-summary-output`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.show-summary-output) | Where to print a summary of the update operation. | `log` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.tag"></a>[`spring.liquibase.tag`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.tag) | Tag name to use when applying database changes. Can also be used with "rollbackFile" to generate a rollback script for all existing changes associated with that tag. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.test-rollback-on-update"></a>[`spring.liquibase.test-rollback-on-update`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.test-rollback-on-update) | Whether rollback should be tested before update is performed. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.ui-service"></a>[`spring.liquibase.ui-service`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.ui-service) | Which UIService to use. | `logger` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.url"></a>[`spring.liquibase.url`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.url) | JDBC URL of the database to migrate. If not set, the primary configured data source is used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.user"></a>[`spring.liquibase.user`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.liquibase.user) | Login user of the database to migrate. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.continue-on-error"></a>[`spring.sql.init.continue-on-error`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.continue-on-error) | Whether initialization should continue when an error occurs. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.data-locations"></a>[`spring.sql.init.data-locations`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.data-locations) | Locations of the data (DML) scripts to apply to the database. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.encoding"></a>[`spring.sql.init.encoding`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.encoding) | Encoding of the schema and data scripts. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.mode"></a>[`spring.sql.init.mode`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.mode) | Mode to apply when determining whether initialization should be performed. | `embedded` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.password"></a>[`spring.sql.init.password`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.password) | Password of the database to use when applying initialization scripts (if different). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.platform"></a>[`spring.sql.init.platform`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.platform) | Platform to use in the default schema or data script locations, schema-${platform}.sql and data-${platform}.sql. | `all` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.schema-locations"></a>[`spring.sql.init.schema-locations`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.schema-locations) | Locations of the schema (DDL) scripts to apply to the database. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.separator"></a>[`spring.sql.init.separator`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.separator) | Statement separator in the schema and data scripts. | `;` |
| <a id="application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.username"></a>[`spring.sql.init.username`](#application-properties.data-migration.spring.sql.init.username) | Username of the database to use when applying initialization scripts (if different). |  |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.data"></a>

## Data Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.compression"></a>[`spring.cassandra.compression`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.compression) | Compression supported by the Cassandra binary protocol. | `none` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.config"></a>[`spring.cassandra.config`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.config) | Location of the configuration file to use. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.connection.connect-timeout"></a>[`spring.cassandra.connection.connect-timeout`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.connection.connect-timeout) | Timeout to use when establishing driver connections. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.connection.init-query-timeout"></a>[`spring.cassandra.connection.init-query-timeout`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.connection.init-query-timeout) | Timeout to use for internal queries that run as part of the initialization process, just after a connection is opened. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.contact-points"></a>[`spring.cassandra.contact-points`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.contact-points) | Cluster node addresses in the form 'host:port', or a simple 'host' to use the configured port. | `[127.0.0.1:9042]` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.controlconnection.timeout"></a>[`spring.cassandra.controlconnection.timeout`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.controlconnection.timeout) | Timeout to use for control queries. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.keyspace-name"></a>[`spring.cassandra.keyspace-name`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.keyspace-name) | Keyspace name to use. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.local-datacenter"></a>[`spring.cassandra.local-datacenter`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.local-datacenter) | Datacenter that is considered "local". Contact points should be from this datacenter. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.password"></a>[`spring.cassandra.password`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.password) | Login password of the server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.pool.heartbeat-interval"></a>[`spring.cassandra.pool.heartbeat-interval`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.pool.heartbeat-interval) | Heartbeat interval after which a message is sent on an idle connection to make sure it's still alive. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.pool.idle-timeout"></a>[`spring.cassandra.pool.idle-timeout`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.pool.idle-timeout) | Idle timeout before an idle connection is removed. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.port"></a>[`spring.cassandra.port`](#application-properties.data.spring.cassandra.port) | Port to use if a contact point does not specify one. | `9042` |

This setting only has an effect if both it and time between eviction runs are positive. | `0` |

When positive, the idle object evictor thread starts, otherwise no idle object eviction is performed. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.enabled"></a>[`spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.enabled`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.enabled) | Whether to enable the pool. Enabled automatically if "commons-pool2" is available. With Jedis, pooling is implicitly enabled in sentinel mode and this setting only applies to single node setup. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-active"></a>[`spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-active`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-active) | Maximum number of connections that can be allocated by the pool at a given time. Use a negative value for no limit. | `8` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-idle"></a>[`spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-idle`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-idle) | Maximum number of "idle" connections in the pool. Use a negative value to indicate an unlimited number of idle connections. | `8` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-wait"></a>[`spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-wait`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.max-wait) | Maximum amount of time a connection allocation should block before throwing an exception when the pool is exhausted. Use a negative value to block indefinitely. | `-1ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.min-idle"></a>[`spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.min-idle`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.min-idle) | Target for the minimum number of idle connections to maintain in the pool. This setting only has an effect if both it and time between eviction runs are positive. | `0` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.time-between-eviction-runs"></a>[`spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.time-between-eviction-runs`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.lettuce.pool.time-between-eviction-runs) | Time between runs of the idle object evictor thread. | <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.delay"></a>[`spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.delay`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.delay) | Base delay for a recovery attempt. Can be combined with a "multiplier" to use an exponential back off strategy. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.jitter"></a>[`spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.jitter`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.jitter) | Jitter value for the base retry attempt, randomly subtracted or added to the calculated delay, resulting in a value between 'delay - jitter' and 'delay + jitter' but never below the base delay or above the max delay. | `0` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.max-delay"></a>[`spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.max-delay`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.max-delay) | Maximum delay for any retry attempt, limiting how far jitter and the multiplier can increase the delay. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.max-retries"></a>[`spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.max-retries`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.max-retries) | Maximum number of recovery attempts. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.multiplier"></a>[`spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.multiplier`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.listener.recovery.multiplier) | Multiplier for a delay for the next retry attempt, applied to the previous delay, starting with the initial delay as well as to the applicable jitter for each attempt. | <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.username"></a>[`spring.data.redis.username`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.redis.username) | Login username of the redis server. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.rest.default-media-type"></a>[`spring.data.rest.default-media-type`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.rest.default-media-type) | Content type to use as a default when none is specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.rest.default-page-size"></a>[`spring.data.rest.default-page-size`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.rest.default-page-size) | Default size of pages. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.data.rest.page-param-name"></a>[`spring.data.rest.page-param-name`](#application-properties.data.spring.data.rest.page-param-name) | Name of the URL query string parameter that indicates what page to return. |  |

Use -1 to use the JDBC driver's default configuration. | `-1` |

When set to false, throw an SQLWarningException instead. | `true` |

Default is to use the JDBC driver's default configuration. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jdbc.template.skip-results-processing"></a>[`spring.jdbc.template.skip-results-processing`](#application-properties.data.spring.jdbc.template.skip-results-processing) | Whether results processing should be skipped. Can be used to optimize callable statement processing when we know that no results are being passed back. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jdbc.template.skip-undeclared-results"></a>[`spring.jdbc.template.skip-undeclared-results`](#application-properties.data.spring.jdbc.template.skip-undeclared-results) | Whether undeclared results should be skipped. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.database"></a>[`spring.jpa.database`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.database) | Target database to operate on, auto-detected by default. Can be alternatively set using the "databasePlatform" property. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.database-platform"></a>[`spring.jpa.database-platform`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.database-platform) | Name of the target database to operate on, auto-detected by default. Can be alternatively set using the "Database" enum. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.defer-datasource-initialization"></a>[`spring.jpa.defer-datasource-initialization`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.defer-datasource-initialization) | Whether to defer DataSource initialization until after any EntityManagerFactory beans have been created and initialized. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto"></a>[`spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto) | DDL mode. This is actually a shortcut for the "hibernate.hbm2ddl.auto" property. Defaults to "create-drop" when using an embedded database and no schema manager was detected. Otherwise, defaults to "none". |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.hibernate.naming.implicit-strategy"></a>[`spring.jpa.hibernate.naming.implicit-strategy`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.hibernate.naming.implicit-strategy) | Fully qualified name of the implicit naming strategy. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.mapping-resources"></a>[`spring.jpa.mapping-resources`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.mapping-resources) | Mapping resources (equivalent to "mapping-file" entries in persistence.xml). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.open-in-view"></a>[`spring.jpa.open-in-view`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.open-in-view) | Register OpenEntityManagerInViewInterceptor. Binds a JPA EntityManager to the thread for the entire processing of the request. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.properties"></a>[`spring.jpa.properties.*`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.properties) | Additional native properties to set on the JPA provider. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.jpa.show-sql"></a>[`spring.jpa.show-sql`](#application-properties.data.spring.jpa.show-sql) | Whether to enable logging of SQL statements. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.ldap.embedded.validation.enabled"></a>[`spring.ldap.embedded.validation.enabled`](#application-properties.data.spring.ldap.embedded.validation.enabled) | Whether to enable LDAP schema validation. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.ldap.embedded.validation.schema"></a>[`spring.ldap.embedded.validation.schema`](#application-properties.data.spring.ldap.embedded.validation.schema) | Path to the custom schema. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.ldap.password"></a>[`spring.ldap.password`](#application-properties.data.spring.ldap.password) | Login password of the server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.ldap.referral"></a>[`spring.ldap.referral`](#application-properties.data.spring.ldap.referral) | Specify how referrals encountered by the service provider are to be processed. If not specified, the default is determined by the provider. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.authentication-database"></a>[`spring.mongodb.authentication-database`](#application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.authentication-database) | Authentication database name. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.host"></a>[`spring.mongodb.host`](#application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.host) | Mongo server host. Ignored if 'uri' is set. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.password"></a>[`spring.mongodb.password`](#application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.password) | Login password of the mongo server. Ignored if 'uri' is set. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.port"></a>[`spring.mongodb.port`](#application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.port) | Mongo server port. Ignored if 'uri' is set. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.replica-set-name"></a>[`spring.mongodb.replica-set-name`](#application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.replica-set-name) | Required replica set name for the cluster. Ignored if 'uri' is set. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.ssl.enabled"></a>[`spring.mongodb.ssl.enabled`](#application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.ssl.enabled) | Whether to enable SSL support. Enabled automatically if "bundle" is provided unless specified otherwise. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.uri"></a>[`spring.mongodb.uri`](#application-properties.data.spring.mongodb.uri) | Mongo database URI. Overrides host, port, username, and password. | `mongodb://localhost/test` |

Mutual exclusive with a given username. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.authentication.password"></a>[`spring.neo4j.authentication.password`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.authentication.password) | Login password of the server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.authentication.realm"></a>[`spring.neo4j.authentication.realm`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.authentication.realm) | Realm to connect to. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.authentication.username"></a>[`spring.neo4j.authentication.username`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.authentication.username) | Login user of the server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.connection-timeout"></a>[`spring.neo4j.connection-timeout`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.connection-timeout) | Timeout for borrowing connections from the pool. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.max-transaction-retry-time"></a>[`spring.neo4j.max-transaction-retry-time`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.max-transaction-retry-time) | Maximum time transactions are allowed to retry. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.connection-acquisition-timeout"></a>[`spring.neo4j.pool.connection-acquisition-timeout`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.connection-acquisition-timeout) | Acquisition of new connections will be attempted for at most configured timeout. | `60s` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.idle-time-before-connection-test"></a>[`spring.neo4j.pool.idle-time-before-connection-test`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.idle-time-before-connection-test) | Pooled connections that have been idle in the pool for longer than this threshold will be tested before they are used again. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.log-leaked-sessions"></a>[`spring.neo4j.pool.log-leaked-sessions`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.log-leaked-sessions) | Whether to log leaked sessions. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.max-connection-lifetime"></a>[`spring.neo4j.pool.max-connection-lifetime`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.max-connection-lifetime) | Pooled connections older than this threshold will be closed and removed from the pool. | `1h` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.max-connection-pool-size"></a>[`spring.neo4j.pool.max-connection-pool-size`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.pool.max-connection-pool-size) | Maximum amount of connections in the connection pool towards a single database. | `100` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.cert-file"></a>[`spring.neo4j.security.cert-file`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.cert-file) | Path to the file that holds the trusted certificates. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.encrypted"></a>[`spring.neo4j.security.encrypted`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.encrypted) | Whether the driver should use encrypted traffic. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.hostname-verification-enabled"></a>[`spring.neo4j.security.hostname-verification-enabled`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.hostname-verification-enabled) | Whether hostname verification is required. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.trust-strategy"></a>[`spring.neo4j.security.trust-strategy`](#application-properties.data.spring.neo4j.security.trust-strategy) | Trust strategy to use. | `trust-system-ca-signed-certificates` |

| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.generate-unique-name"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.generate-unique-name`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.generate-unique-name) | Whether to generate a random database name. Ignore any configured name when enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.name"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.name`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.name) | Database name. Set if no name is specified in the url. Default to "testdb" when using an embedded database. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.password"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.password`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.password) | Login password of the database. Set if no password is specified in the url. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.acquire-retry"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.acquire-retry`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.acquire-retry) | Number of acquire retries if the first acquire attempt fails. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.enabled"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.enabled`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.enabled) | Whether pooling is enabled. Requires r2dbc-pool. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.initial-size"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.initial-size`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.initial-size) | Initial connection pool size. | `10` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-acquire-time"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.max-acquire-time`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-acquire-time) | Maximum time to acquire a connection from the pool. By default, wait indefinitely. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-create-connection-time"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.max-create-connection-time`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-create-connection-time) | Maximum time to wait to create a new connection. By default, wait indefinitely. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-idle-time"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.max-idle-time`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-idle-time) | Maximum amount of time that a connection is allowed to sit idle in the pool. | `30m` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-life-time"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.max-life-time`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-life-time) | Maximum lifetime of a connection in the pool. By default, connections have an infinite lifetime. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-size"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.max-size`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-size) | Maximal connection pool size. | `10` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-validation-time"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.max-validation-time`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.max-validation-time) | Maximum time to validate a connection from the pool. By default, wait indefinitely. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.min-idle"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.min-idle`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.min-idle) | Minimal number of idle connections. | `0` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.validation-depth"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.validation-depth`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.validation-depth) | Validation depth. | `local` |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.validation-query"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.pool.validation-query`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.pool.validation-query) | Validation query. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.properties"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.properties.*`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.properties) | Additional R2DBC options. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.url"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.url`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.url) | R2DBC URL of the database. database name, username, password and pooling options specified in the url take precedence over individual options. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.username"></a>[`spring.r2dbc.username`](#application-properties.data.spring.r2dbc.username) | Login username of the database. Set if no username is specified in the url. |  |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.devtools"></a>

## Devtools Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.add-properties"></a>[`spring.devtools.add-properties`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.add-properties) | Whether to enable development property defaults. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.context-path"></a>[`spring.devtools.remote.context-path`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.context-path) | Context path used to handle the remote connection. | `/.~~spring-boot!~` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.proxy.host"></a>[`spring.devtools.remote.proxy.host`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.proxy.host) | The host of the proxy to use to connect to the remote application. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.proxy.port"></a>[`spring.devtools.remote.proxy.port`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.proxy.port) | The port of the proxy to use to connect to the remote application. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.restart.enabled"></a>[`spring.devtools.remote.restart.enabled`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.restart.enabled) | Whether to enable remote restart. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.secret"></a>[`spring.devtools.remote.secret`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.secret) | A shared secret required to establish a connection (required to enable remote support). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.secret-header-name"></a>[`spring.devtools.remote.secret-header-name`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.remote.secret-header-name) | HTTP header used to transfer the shared secret. | `X-AUTH-TOKEN` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.additional-exclude"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.additional-exclude`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.additional-exclude) | Additional patterns that should be excluded from triggering a full restart. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.additional-paths"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.additional-paths`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.additional-paths) | Additional paths to watch for changes. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.enabled"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.enabled`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.enabled) | Whether to enable automatic restart. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.exclude"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.exclude`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.exclude) | Patterns that should be excluded from triggering a full restart. | `META-INF/maven/**,META-INF/resources/**,resources/**,static/**,public/**,templates/**,**/*Test.class,**/*Tests.class,git.properties,META-INF/build-info.properties` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.log-condition-evaluation-delta"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.log-condition-evaluation-delta`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.log-condition-evaluation-delta) | Whether to log the condition evaluation delta upon restart. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.poll-interval"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.poll-interval`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.poll-interval) | Amount of time to wait between polling for classpath changes. | `1s` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.quiet-period"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.quiet-period`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.quiet-period) | Amount of quiet time required without any classpath changes before a restart is triggered. | `400ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.trigger-file"></a>[`spring.devtools.restart.trigger-file`](#application-properties.devtools.spring.devtools.restart.trigger-file) | Name of a specific file that, when changed, triggers the restart check. Must be a simple name (without any path) of a file that appears on your classpath. If not specified, any classpath file change triggers the restart. |  |

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |

| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.host"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.host`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.host) | Hostname or IP of the machine where the docker containers are started. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.profiles.active"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.profiles.active`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.profiles.active) | Docker compose profiles that should be active. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.tcp.connect-timeout"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.readiness.tcp.connect-timeout`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.tcp.connect-timeout) | Timeout for connections. | `200ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.tcp.read-timeout"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.readiness.tcp.read-timeout`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.tcp.read-timeout) | Timeout for reads. | `200ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.timeout"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.readiness.timeout`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.timeout) | Timeout of the readiness checks. | `2m` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.wait"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.readiness.wait`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.readiness.wait) | Wait strategy to use. | `always` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.skip.in-tests"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.skip.in-tests`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.skip.in-tests) | Whether to skip in tests. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.arguments"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.start.arguments`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.arguments) | Arguments to pass to the start command. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.command"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.start.command`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.command) | Command used to start Docker Compose. | `up` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.log-level"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.start.log-level`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.log-level) | Log level for output. | `info` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.skip"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.start.skip`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.start.skip) | Whether to skip executing the start command. | `if-running` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.stop.arguments"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.stop.arguments`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.stop.arguments) | Arguments to pass to the stop command. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.stop.command"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.stop.command`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.stop.command) | Command used to stop Docker Compose. | `stop` |
| <a id="application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.stop.timeout"></a>[`spring.docker.compose.stop.timeout`](#application-properties.docker-compose.spring.docker.compose.stop.timeout) | Timeout for stopping Docker Compose. Use '0' for forced stop. | `10s` |
