| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.channel"></a>[`spring.grpc.client.channel.*`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.channel) | Map of channels configured by name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.channelfactory.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.client.channelfactory.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.channelfactory.enabled) | Whether to enable gRPC channel factory bean auto-configuration. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.client.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.enabled) | Whether to enable gRPC client auto-configuration. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.inprocess.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.client.inprocess.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.inprocess.enabled) | Whether to configure the in-process channel factory. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.observation.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.client.observation.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.client.observation.enabled) | Whether to enable Observations on the client. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.address"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.address`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.address) | Network address to which the gRPC server should bind. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.enabled) | Whether to enable gRPC server auto-configuration. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.factory.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.factory.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.factory.enabled) | Whether to enable gRPC server factory bean auto-configuration. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.enabled) | Whether to auto-configure the Health feature on the gRPC server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.include-overall-health"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.include-overall-health`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.include-overall-health) | Whether to include the overall server health. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.delay"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.delay`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.delay) | Initial delay before updating the health status the very first time. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.enabled) | Whether to schedule updates to gRPC server health based on application health. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.period"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.period`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.schedule.period) | How often to update the health status. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.service"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.service.*`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.service) | Service-specific health reporting. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.services.validate-membership"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.services.validate-membership`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.services.validate-membership) | Whether to validate health membership for services on startup. Validation fails if a service includes or excludes a health contributor that does not exist. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.status.mapping"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.status.mapping.*`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.status.mapping) | Mapping of health statuses to gRPC service status. By default, registered health statuses map to sensible defaults (for example, UP maps to SERVING). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.status.order"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.health.status.order`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.health.status.order) | List of health statuses in order of severity. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.inbound.message.max-size"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.inbound.message.max-size`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.inbound.message.max-size) | Maximum message size allowed to be received by the server. If a data size suffix is not specified, bytes will be used. | `4194304B` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.inbound.metadata.max-size"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.inbound.metadata.max-size`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.inbound.metadata.max-size) | Maximum metadata size allowed to be received by the server. If a data size suffix is not specified, bytes will be used. | `8192B` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.inprocess.name"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.inprocess.name`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.inprocess.name) | Name of the in-process server or null to not start the in-process server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.grace-period"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.grace-period`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.grace-period) | Maximum time for graceful connection termination. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.max-age"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.max-age`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.max-age) | Maximum time a connection may exist before being gracefully terminated (infinite if not specified). If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.max-idle-time"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.max-idle-time`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.connection.max-idle-time) | Maximum time a connection can remain idle before being gracefully terminated (infinite if not specified). If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.permit.time"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.keepalive.permit.time`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.permit.time) | Maximum keep-alive time clients are permitted to configure. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `5m` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.permit.without-calls"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.keepalive.permit.without-calls`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.permit.without-calls) | Whether clients are permitted to send keep alive pings when there are no outstanding RPCs on the connection. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.time"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.keepalive.time`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.time) | Duration without read activity before sending a keep alive ping. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `2h` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.timeout"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.keepalive.timeout`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.keepalive.timeout) | Maximum time to wait for read activity after sending a keep alive ping. If the sender does not receive an acknowledgment within this time, it will close the connection. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `20s` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.netty.domain-socket-path"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.netty.domain-socket-path`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.netty.domain-socket-path) | Path of the domain socket that should be used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.netty.transport"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.netty.transport`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.netty.transport) | Transport mechanism used for Netty and Netty Shaded servers. If not specified, the appropriate transport will be picked based on the 'domain-socket-path' or 'address/port'. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.observation.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.observation.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.observation.enabled) | Whether to enable Observations on the server. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.port"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.port`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.port) | Port on which the gRPC server should listen. Use '0' to bind to a dynamic port. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.reflection.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.reflection.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.reflection.enabled) | Whether to enable Reflection on the gRPC server. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.security.csrf.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.security.csrf.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.security.csrf.enabled) | Whether to enable CSRF protection. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.servlet.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.servlet.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.servlet.enabled) | Whether to use a servlet server in a servlet-based web application. When the value is false, a native gRPC server will be created as long as one is available, and it will listen on its own port. Should only be needed if the GrpcServlet is on the classpath. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.servlet.validate-http2"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.servlet.validate-http2`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.servlet.validate-http2) | Whether to validate that HTTP/2 is enabled. Validation may need to be skipped if your servlet container is not configured using properties. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.shutdown.grace-period"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.shutdown.grace-period`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.shutdown.grace-period) | Maximum time to wait for the server to gracefully shutdown. When the value is negative, the server waits forever. When the value is 0, the server will force shutdown immediately. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.bundle"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.ssl.bundle`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.bundle) | SSL bundle name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.client-auth"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.ssl.client-auth`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.client-auth) | Client authentication mode. | `none` |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.enabled"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.ssl.enabled`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.enabled) | Whether to enable SSL support. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.secure"></a>[`spring.grpc.server.ssl.secure`](#application-properties.grpc.spring.grpc.server.ssl.secure) | Whether client authentication is secure (that is certificates are checked). Do not set this to false in production. | `true` |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.integration"></a>

## Integration Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.broker-url"></a>[`spring.activemq.broker-url`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.broker-url) | URL of the ActiveMQ broker. Auto-generated by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.close-timeout"></a>[`spring.activemq.close-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.close-timeout) | Time to wait before considering a close complete. | `15s` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.embedded.enabled"></a>[`spring.activemq.embedded.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.embedded.enabled) | Whether to enable embedded mode if the ActiveMQ Broker is available. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.non-blocking-redelivery"></a>[`spring.activemq.non-blocking-redelivery`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.non-blocking-redelivery) | Whether to stop message delivery before re-delivering messages from a rolled back transaction. This implies that message order is not preserved when this is enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.packages.trust-all"></a>[`spring.activemq.packages.trust-all`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.packages.trust-all) | Whether to trust all packages. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.packages.trusted"></a>[`spring.activemq.packages.trusted`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.packages.trusted) | List of specific packages to trust (when not trusting all packages). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.password"></a>[`spring.activemq.password`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.password) | Login password of the broker. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.block-if-full"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.block-if-full`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.block-if-full) | Whether to block when a connection is requested and the pool is full. Set it to false to throw a "JMSException" instead. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.block-if-full-timeout"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.block-if-full-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.block-if-full-timeout) | Blocking period before throwing an exception if the pool is still full. | `-1ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.enabled"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.enabled) | Whether a JmsPoolConnectionFactory should be created, instead of a regular ConnectionFactory. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.idle-timeout"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.idle-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.idle-timeout) | Connection idle timeout. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.max-connections"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.max-connections`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.max-connections) | Maximum number of pooled connections. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.max-sessions-per-connection"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.max-sessions-per-connection`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.max-sessions-per-connection) | Maximum number of pooled sessions per connection in the pool. | `500` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.time-between-expiration-check"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.time-between-expiration-check`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.time-between-expiration-check) | Time to sleep between runs of the idle connection eviction thread. When negative, no idle connection eviction thread runs. | `-1ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.use-anonymous-producers"></a>[`spring.activemq.pool.use-anonymous-producers`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.pool.use-anonymous-producers) | Whether to use only one anonymous "MessageProducer" instance. Set it to false to create one "MessageProducer" every time one is required. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.send-timeout"></a>[`spring.activemq.send-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.send-timeout) | Time to wait on message sends for a response. Set it to 0 to wait forever. | `0ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.user"></a>[`spring.activemq.user`](#application-properties.integration.spring.activemq.user) | Login user of the broker. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.broker-url"></a>[`spring.artemis.broker-url`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.broker-url) | Artemis broker url. | `tcp://localhost:61616` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.cluster-password"></a>[`spring.artemis.embedded.cluster-password`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.cluster-password) | Cluster password. Randomly generated on startup by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.data-directory"></a>[`spring.artemis.embedded.data-directory`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.data-directory) | Journal file directory. Not necessary if persistence is turned off. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.enabled"></a>[`spring.artemis.embedded.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.enabled) | Whether to enable embedded mode if the Artemis server APIs are available. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.persistent"></a>[`spring.artemis.embedded.persistent`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.persistent) | Whether to enable persistent store. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.server-id"></a>[`spring.artemis.embedded.server-id`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.server-id) | Server ID. By default, an auto-incremented counter is used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.topics"></a>[`spring.artemis.embedded.topics`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.embedded.topics) | List of topics to create on startup. | `[]` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.mode"></a>[`spring.artemis.mode`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.mode) | Artemis deployment mode, auto-detected by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.password"></a>[`spring.artemis.password`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.password) | Login password of the broker. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.block-if-full"></a>[`spring.artemis.pool.block-if-full`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.block-if-full) | Whether to block when a connection is requested and the pool is full. Set it to false to throw a "JMSException" instead. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.block-if-full-timeout"></a>[`spring.artemis.pool.block-if-full-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.block-if-full-timeout) | Blocking period before throwing an exception if the pool is still full. | `-1ms` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.idle-timeout"></a>[`spring.artemis.pool.idle-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.idle-timeout) | Connection idle timeout. | `30s` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.max-connections"></a>[`spring.artemis.pool.max-connections`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.max-connections) | Maximum number of pooled connections. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.max-sessions-per-connection"></a>[`spring.artemis.pool.max-sessions-per-connection`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.max-sessions-per-connection) | Maximum number of pooled sessions per connection in the pool. | `500` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.time-between-expiration-check"></a>[`spring.artemis.pool.time-between-expiration-check`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.time-between-expiration-check) | Time to sleep between runs of the idle connection eviction thread. When negative, no idle connection eviction thread runs. | `-1ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.use-anonymous-producers"></a>[`spring.artemis.pool.use-anonymous-producers`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.pool.use-anonymous-producers) | Whether to use only one anonymous "MessageProducer" instance. Set it to false to create one "MessageProducer" every time one is required. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.user"></a>[`spring.artemis.user`](#application-properties.integration.spring.artemis.user) | Login user of the broker. |  |

The backup cluster is chosen in the sequence of the given list. If all backup clusters are available, the Pulsar client chooses the first backup cluster. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.failover.check-interval"></a>[`spring.pulsar.client.failover.check-interval`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.failover.check-interval) | Frequency of performing a probe task. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.lookup-timeout"></a>[`spring.pulsar.client.lookup-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.lookup-timeout) | Client lookup timeout. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.threads.io"></a>[`spring.pulsar.client.threads.io`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.threads.io) | Number of threads to be used for handling connections to brokers. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.threads.listener"></a>[`spring.pulsar.client.threads.listener`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.client.threads.listener) | Number of threads to be used for message listeners. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.dead-letter-policy.initial-subscription-name"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.dead-letter-policy.initial-subscription-name`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.dead-letter-policy.initial-subscription-name) | Name of the initial subscription of the dead letter topic. When not set, the initial subscription will not be created. However, when the property is set then the broker's 'allowAutoSubscriptionCreation' must be enabled or the DLQ producer will fail. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.dead-letter-policy.max-redeliver-count"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.dead-letter-policy.max-redeliver-count`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.dead-letter-policy.max-redeliver-count) | Maximum number of times that a message will be redelivered before being sent to the dead letter queue. | `0` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.priority-level"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.priority-level`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.priority-level) | Priority level for shared subscription consumers. | `0` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.mode"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.mode`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.mode) | Subscription mode to be used when subscribing to the topic. | `durable` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.name"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.name`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.name) | Subscription name for the consumer. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.topics-mode"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.topics-mode`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.topics-mode) | Determines which type of topics (persistent, non-persistent, or all) the consumer should be subscribed to when using pattern subscriptions. | `persistentonly` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.type"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.type`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.subscription.type) | Subscription type to be used when subscribing to a topic. | `exclusive` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.topics-pattern"></a>[`spring.pulsar.consumer.topics-pattern`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.consumer.topics-pattern) | Pattern for topics the consumer subscribes to. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.enabled"></a>[`spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.enabled) | Whether to enable default tenant and namespace support for topics. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.namespace"></a>[`spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.namespace`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.namespace) | Default namespace to use when producing or consuming messages against a non-fully-qualified topic URL. | `default` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.tenant"></a>[`spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.tenant`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.topic.tenant) | Default tenant to use when producing or consuming messages against a non-fully-qualified topic URL. | `public` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.type-mappings"></a>[`spring.pulsar.defaults.type-mappings`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.defaults.type-mappings) | List of mappings from message type to topic name and schema info to use as a defaults when a topic name and/or schema is not explicitly specified when producing or consuming messages of the mapped type. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.function.enabled"></a>[`spring.pulsar.function.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.function.enabled) | Whether to enable function support. | `true` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.function.propagate-failures"></a>[`spring.pulsar.function.propagate-failures`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.function.propagate-failures) | Whether to throw an exception if any failure is encountered during server startup while creating/updating functions. | `true` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.listener.observation-enabled"></a>[`spring.pulsar.listener.observation-enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.listener.observation-enabled) | Whether to record observations for when the Observations API is available and the client supports it. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.producer.hashing-scheme"></a>[`spring.pulsar.producer.hashing-scheme`](#application-properties.integration.spring.pulsar.producer.hashing-scheme) | Message hashing scheme to choose the partition to which the message is published. | `javastringhash` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.direct.auto-startup"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.direct.auto-startup`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.direct.auto-startup) | Whether to start the container automatically on startup. | `true` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.direct.retry.initial-interval"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.direct.retry.initial-interval`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.direct.retry.initial-interval) | Duration between the first and second attempt to deliver a message. | `1000ms` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.auto-startup"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.auto-startup`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.auto-startup) | Whether to start the container automatically on startup. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.batch-size"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.batch-size`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.batch-size) | Batch size, expressed as the number of physical messages, to be used by the container. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.consumer-batch-enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.consumer-batch-enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.consumer-batch-enabled) | Whether the container creates a batch of messages based on the 'receive-timeout' and 'batch-size'. | <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.de-batching-enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.de-batching-enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.de-batching-enabled) | Whether the container should present batched messages as discrete messages or call the listener with the batch. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.default-requeue-rejected"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.default-requeue-rejected`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.default-requeue-rejected) | Whether rejected deliveries are re-queued by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.force-stop"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.force-stop`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.force-stop) | Whether the container (when stopped) should stop immediately after processing the current message or stop after processing all pre-fetched messages. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.idle-event-interval"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.idle-event-interval`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.idle-event-interval) | How often idle container events should be published. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.max-concurrency"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.max-concurrency`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.max-concurrency) | Maximum number of listener invoker threads. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.missing-queues-fatal"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.missing-queues-fatal`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.missing-queues-fatal) | Whether to fail if the queues declared by the container are not available on the broker and/or whether to stop the container if one or more queues are deleted at runtime. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.observation-enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.observation-enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.observation-enabled) | Whether to enable observation. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.prefetch"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.prefetch`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.prefetch) | Maximum number of unacknowledged messages that can be outstanding at each consumer. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.enabled) | Whether publishing retries are enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.initial-interval"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.initial-interval`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.initial-interval) | Duration between the first and second attempt to deliver a message. | `1000ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.max-interval"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.max-interval`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.max-interval) | Maximum duration between attempts. | `10000ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.max-retries"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.max-retries`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.max-retries) | Maximum number of retry attempts to deliver a message. | `3` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.multiplier"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.multiplier`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.multiplier) | Multiplier to apply to the previous retry interval. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.stateless"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.stateless`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.simple.retry.stateless) | Whether retries are stateless or stateful. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.stream.native-listener"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.stream.native-listener`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.stream.native-listener) | Whether the container will support listeners that consume native stream messages instead of Spring AMQP messages. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.stream.observation-enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.stream.observation-enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.stream.observation-enabled) | Whether to enable observation. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.type"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.listener.type`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.listener.type) | Listener container type. | `simple` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.max-inbound-message-body-size"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.max-inbound-message-body-size`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.max-inbound-message-body-size) | Maximum size of the body of inbound (received) messages. | `64MB` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.password"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.password`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.password) | Login to authenticate against the broker. | `guest` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.publisher-confirm-type"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.publisher-confirm-type`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.publisher-confirm-type) | Type of publisher confirms to use. |  |

Determined automatically if an address is provided with the protocol (amqp:// vs. amqps://). |  |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store) | Trust store that holds SSL certificates. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-algorithm"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-algorithm`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-algorithm) | Trust store algorithm. | `SunX509` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-password"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-password`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-password) | Password used to access the trust store. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-type"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-type`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.trust-store-type) | Trust store type. | `JKS` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.validate-server-certificate"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.ssl.validate-server-certificate`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.validate-server-certificate) | Whether to enable server side certificate validation. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.verify-hostname"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.ssl.verify-hostname`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.ssl.verify-hostname) | Whether to enable hostname verification. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.host"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.host`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.host) | Host of a RabbitMQ instance with the Stream plugin enabled. | `localhost` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.name"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.name`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.name) | Name of the stream. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.password"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.password`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.password) | Login password to authenticate to the broker. When not set spring.rabbitmq.password is used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.port"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.port`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.port) | Stream port of a RabbitMQ instance with the Stream plugin enabled. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.ssl.bundle"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.ssl.bundle`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.ssl.bundle) | SSL bundle name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.ssl.enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.ssl.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.ssl.enabled) | Whether to enable SSL support. Enabled automatically if "bundle" is provided. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.username"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.username`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.username) | Login user to authenticate to the broker. When not set, spring.rabbitmq.username is used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.virtual-host"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.stream.virtual-host`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.stream.virtual-host) | Virtual host of a RabbitMQ instance with the Stream plugin enabled. When not set, spring.rabbitmq.virtual-host is used. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.default-receive-queue"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.default-receive-queue`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.default-receive-queue) | Name of the default queue to receive messages from when none is specified explicitly. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.mandatory"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.mandatory`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.mandatory) | Whether to enable mandatory messages. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.observation-enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.observation-enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.observation-enabled) | Whether to enable observation. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.reply-timeout"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.reply-timeout`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.reply-timeout) | Timeout for sendAndReceive() operations. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.enabled"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.enabled`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.enabled) | Whether publishing retries are enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.initial-interval"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.initial-interval`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.initial-interval) | Duration between the first and second attempt to deliver a message. | `1000ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.max-interval"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.max-interval`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.max-interval) | Maximum duration between attempts. | `10000ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.max-retries"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.max-retries`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.max-retries) | Maximum number of retry attempts to deliver a message. | `3` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.multiplier"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.multiplier`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.retry.multiplier) | Multiplier to apply to the previous retry interval. | `1` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.routing-key"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.template.routing-key`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.template.routing-key) | Value of a default routing key to use for send operations. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.username"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.username`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.username) | Login user to authenticate to the broker. | `guest` |
| <a id="application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.virtual-host"></a>[`spring.rabbitmq.virtual-host`](#application-properties.integration.spring.rabbitmq.virtual-host) | Virtual host to use when connecting to the broker. |  |

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.gson.date-format"></a>[`spring.gson.date-format`](#application-properties.json.spring.gson.date-format) | Format to use when serializing Date objects. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.gson.enable-complex-map-key-serialization"></a>[`spring.gson.enable-complex-map-key-serialization`](#application-properties.json.spring.gson.enable-complex-map-key-serialization) | Whether to enable serialization of complex map keys (i.e. non-primitives). |  |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.gson.exclude-fields-without-expose-annotation"></a>[`spring.gson.exclude-fields-without-expose-annotation`](#application-properties.json.spring.gson.exclude-fields-without-expose-annotation) | Whether to exclude all fields from consideration for serialization or deserialization that do not have the "Expose" annotation. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.gson.field-naming-policy"></a>[`spring.gson.field-naming-policy`](#application-properties.json.spring.gson.field-naming-policy) | Naming policy that should be applied to an object's field during serialization and deserialization. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.gson.generate-non-executable-json"></a>[`spring.gson.generate-non-executable-json`](#application-properties.json.spring.gson.generate-non-executable-json) | Whether to generate non-executable JSON by prefixing the output with some special text. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.gson.pretty-printing"></a>[`spring.gson.pretty-printing`](#application-properties.json.spring.gson.pretty-printing) | Whether to output serialized JSON that fits in a page for pretty printing. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.gson.serialize-nulls"></a>[`spring.gson.serialize-nulls`](#application-properties.json.spring.gson.serialize-nulls) | Whether to serialize null fields. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-comments"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-comments`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-comments) | Whether to allow parser to accept C/Java-style comments in JSON input. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-special-floating-point-values"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-special-floating-point-values`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-special-floating-point-values) | Whether to remove the JSON specification restriction on special floating-point values such as 'NaN' and 'Infinity' and allow their serialization and deserialization as float literals without quotes. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-structured-map-keys"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-structured-map-keys`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-structured-map-keys) | Whether to allow structured objects to be serialized as map keys by changing the serialized form of the map from JSON object (key-value pairs) to flat array like \[k1, v1, k2, v2\]. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-trailing-comma"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-trailing-comma`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.allow-trailing-comma) | Whether to allow parser to accept trailing commas in JSON objects and arrays. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.class-discriminator"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.class-discriminator`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.class-discriminator) | Name of the class descriptor property for polymorphic serialization. | `type` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.class-discriminator-mode"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.class-discriminator-mode`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.class-discriminator-mode) | Defines which classes and objects should have class discriminator added to the output. | `polymorphic` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.coerce-input-values"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.coerce-input-values`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.coerce-input-values) | Whether to coerce incorrect JSON values. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.decode-enums-case-insensitive"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.decode-enums-case-insensitive`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.decode-enums-case-insensitive) | Whether enum values are decoded in a case-insensitive manner. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.encode-defaults"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.encode-defaults`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.encode-defaults) | Whether default values of Kotlin properties should be encoded. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.explicit-nulls"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.explicit-nulls`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.explicit-nulls) | Whether null values should be encoded for nullable properties and must be present in JSON object during decoding. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.ignore-unknown-keys"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.ignore-unknown-keys`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.ignore-unknown-keys) | Whether encounters of unknown properties in the input JSON should be ignored instead of throwing SerializationException. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.lenient"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.lenient`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.lenient) | Whether parser should operate in lenient mode, removing the JSON specification restriction (RFC-4627) and being more liberal to malformed input. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.naming-strategy"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.naming-strategy`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.naming-strategy) | Specifies JsonNamingStrategy that should be used for all properties in classes for serialization and deserialization. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.pretty-print"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.pretty-print`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.pretty-print) | Whether resulting JSON should be pretty-printed. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.use-alternative-names"></a>[`spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.use-alternative-names`](#application-properties.json.spring.kotlinx.serialization.json.use-alternative-names) | Whether Json instance makes use of JsonNames annotation. | `true` |

If set, 'mail.(protocol).ssl.socketFactory' property is set to an SSLSocketFactory obtained from the corresponding SSL bundle. <p> Note that the STARTTLS command can use the corresponding SSLSocketFactory, even if the 'mail.(protocol).ssl.enable' property is not set. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.mail.spring.mail.ssl.enabled"></a>[`spring.mail.ssl.enabled`](#application-properties.mail.spring.mail.ssl.enabled) | Whether to enable SSL support. If enabled, 'mail.(protocol).ssl.enable' property is set to 'true'. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.mail.spring.mail.test-connection"></a>[`spring.mail.test-connection`](#application-properties.mail.spring.mail.test-connection) | Whether to test that the mail server is available on startup. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.mail.spring.mail.username"></a>[`spring.mail.username`](#application-properties.mail.spring.mail.username) | Login user of the SMTP server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.mail.spring.sendgrid.api-key"></a>[`spring.sendgrid.api-key`](#application-properties.mail.spring.sendgrid.api-key) | SendGrid API key. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.mail.spring.sendgrid.proxy.host"></a>[`spring.sendgrid.proxy.host`](#application-properties.mail.spring.sendgrid.proxy.host) | SendGrid proxy host. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.mail.spring.sendgrid.proxy.port"></a>[`spring.sendgrid.proxy.port`](#application-properties.mail.spring.sendgrid.proxy.port) | SendGrid proxy port. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.security.spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.client-id"></a>[`spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.client-id`](#application-properties.security.spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.client-id) | Client id used to authenticate with the token introspection endpoint. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.security.spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.client-secret"></a>[`spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.client-secret`](#application-properties.security.spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.client-secret) | Client secret used to authenticate with the token introspection endpoint. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.security.spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.introspection-uri"></a>[`spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.introspection-uri`](#application-properties.security.spring.security.oauth2.resourceserver.opaquetoken.introspection-uri) | OAuth 2.0 endpoint through which token introspection is accomplished. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.security.spring.security.saml2.relyingparty.registration"></a>[`spring.security.saml2.relyingparty.registration.*`](#application-properties.security.spring.security.saml2.relyingparty.registration) | SAML2 relying party registrations. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.security.spring.security.user.name"></a>[`spring.security.user.name`](#application-properties.security.spring.security.user.name) | Default user name. | `user` |
| <a id="application-properties.security.spring.security.user.password"></a>[`spring.security.user.password`](#application-properties.security.spring.security.user.password) | Password for the default user name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.security.spring.security.user.roles"></a>[`spring.security.user.roles`](#application-properties.security.spring.security.user.roles) | Granted roles for the default user name. |  |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.server"></a>

## Server Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.address"></a>[`server.address`](#application-properties.server.server.address) | Network address to which the server should bind. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.compression.additional-mime-types"></a>[`server.compression.additional-mime-types`](#application-properties.server.server.compression.additional-mime-types) | Comma-separated list of additional MIME types that should be compressed. | `[]` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.compression.enabled"></a>[`server.compression.enabled`](#application-properties.server.server.compression.enabled) | Whether response compression is enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.compression.excluded-user-agents"></a>[`server.compression.excluded-user-agents`](#application-properties.server.server.compression.excluded-user-agents) | Comma-separated list of user agents for which responses should not be compressed. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.compression.mime-types"></a>[`server.compression.mime-types`](#application-properties.server.server.compression.mime-types) | Comma-separated list of MIME types that should be compressed. | `[text/html, text/xml, text/plain, text/css, text/javascript, application/javascript, application/json, application/xml]` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.compression.min-response-size"></a>[`server.compression.min-response-size`](#application-properties.server.server.compression.min-response-size) | Minimum "Content-Length" value that is required for compression to be performed. | `2KB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.forward-headers-strategy"></a>[`server.forward-headers-strategy`](#application-properties.server.server.forward-headers-strategy) | Strategy for handling X-Forwarded-\* headers. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.http2.enabled"></a>[`server.http2.enabled`](#application-properties.server.server.http2.enabled) | Whether to enable HTTP/2 support, if the current environment supports it. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.append"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.append`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.append) | Append to log. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.custom-format"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.custom-format`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.custom-format) | Custom log format, see org.eclipse.jetty.server.CustomRequestLog. If defined, overrides the "format" configuration key. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.enabled"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.enabled`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.enabled) | Enable access log. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.file-date-format"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.file-date-format`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.file-date-format) | Date format to place in log file name. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.filename"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.filename`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.filename) | Log filename. If not specified, logs redirect to "System.err". |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.format"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.format`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.format) | Log format. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.ignore-paths"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.ignore-paths`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.ignore-paths) | Request paths that should not be logged. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.retention-period"></a>[`server.jetty.accesslog.retention-period`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.accesslog.retention-period) | Number of days before rotated log files are deleted. | `31` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.connection-idle-timeout"></a>[`server.jetty.connection-idle-timeout`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.connection-idle-timeout) | Time that the connection can be idle before it is closed. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-connections"></a>[`server.jetty.max-connections`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-connections) | Maximum number of connections that the server accepts and processes at any given time. | `-1` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-form-keys"></a>[`server.jetty.max-form-keys`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-form-keys) | Maximum number of form keys. | `1000` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-http-form-post-size"></a>[`server.jetty.max-http-form-post-size`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-http-form-post-size) | Maximum size of the form content in any HTTP post request. | `200000B` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-http-response-header-size"></a>[`server.jetty.max-http-response-header-size`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.max-http-response-header-size) | Maximum size of the HTTP response header. | `16KB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.acceptors"></a>[`server.jetty.threads.acceptors`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.acceptors) | Number of acceptor threads to use. When the value is -1, the default, the number of acceptors is derived from the operating environment. | `-1` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.idle-timeout"></a>[`server.jetty.threads.idle-timeout`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.idle-timeout) | Maximum thread idle time. | `60000ms` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.max"></a>[`server.jetty.threads.max`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.max) | Maximum number of threads. | `200` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.max-queue-capacity"></a>[`server.jetty.threads.max-queue-capacity`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.max-queue-capacity) | Maximum capacity of the thread pool's backing queue. A default is computed based on the threading configuration. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.min"></a>[`server.jetty.threads.min`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.min) | Minimum number of threads. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. | `8` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.selectors"></a>[`server.jetty.threads.selectors`](#application-properties.server.server.jetty.threads.selectors) | Number of selector threads to use. When the value is -1, the default, the number of selectors is derived from the operating environment. | `-1` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.max-http-request-header-size"></a>[`server.max-http-request-header-size`](#application-properties.server.server.max-http-request-header-size) | Maximum size of the HTTP request header. Refer to the documentation for your chosen embedded server for details of exactly how this limit is applied. For example, Netty applies the limit separately to each individual header in the request whereas Tomcat applies the limit to the combined size of the request line and all of the header names and values in the request. | `8KB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.netty.connection-timeout"></a>[`server.netty.connection-timeout`](#application-properties.server.server.netty.connection-timeout) | Connection timeout of the Netty channel. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.netty.h2c-max-content-length"></a>[`server.netty.h2c-max-content-length`](#application-properties.server.server.netty.h2c-max-content-length) | Maximum content length of an H2C upgrade request. | `0B` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.netty.idle-timeout"></a>[`server.netty.idle-timeout`](#application-properties.server.server.netty.idle-timeout) | Idle timeout of the Netty channel. When not specified, an infinite timeout is used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.netty.initial-buffer-size"></a>[`server.netty.initial-buffer-size`](#application-properties.server.server.netty.initial-buffer-size) | Initial buffer size for HTTP request decoding. | `128B` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.netty.max-initial-line-length"></a>[`server.netty.max-initial-line-length`](#application-properties.server.server.netty.max-initial-line-length) | Maximum length that can be decoded for an HTTP request's initial line. | `4KB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.netty.max-keep-alive-requests"></a>[`server.netty.max-keep-alive-requests`](#application-properties.server.server.netty.max-keep-alive-requests) | Maximum number of requests that can be made per connection. By default, a connection serves unlimited number of requests. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.netty.validate-headers"></a>[`server.netty.validate-headers`](#application-properties.server.server.netty.validate-headers) | Whether to validate headers when decoding requests. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.port"></a>[`server.port`](#application-properties.server.server.port) | Server HTTP port. | `8080` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.domain"></a>[`server.reactive.session.cookie.domain`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.domain) | Domain for the cookie. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.http-only"></a>[`server.reactive.session.cookie.http-only`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.http-only) | Whether to use "HttpOnly" cookies for the cookie. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.max-age"></a>[`server.reactive.session.cookie.max-age`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.max-age) | Maximum age of the cookie. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. A positive value indicates when the cookie expires relative to the current time. A value of 0 means the cookie should expire immediately. A negative value means no "Max-Age". |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.name"></a>[`server.reactive.session.cookie.name`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.name) | Name for the cookie. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.partitioned"></a>[`server.reactive.session.cookie.partitioned`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.partitioned) | Whether the generated cookie carries the Partitioned attribute. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.same-site"></a>[`server.reactive.session.cookie.same-site`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.cookie.same-site) | SameSite setting for the cookie. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.max-sessions"></a>[`server.reactive.session.max-sessions`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.max-sessions) | Maximum number of sessions that can be stored. | `10000` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.timeout"></a>[`server.reactive.session.timeout`](#application-properties.server.server.reactive.session.timeout) | Session timeout. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `30m` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.server-header"></a>[`server.server-header`](#application-properties.server.server.server-header) | Value to use for the Server response header (if empty, no header is sent). |  |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.cookie.max-age"></a>[`server.servlet.session.cookie.max-age`](#application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.cookie.max-age) | Maximum age of the cookie. A negative value means no "Max-Age". |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.cookie.name"></a>[`server.servlet.session.cookie.name`](#application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.cookie.name) | Name for the cookie. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.cookie.partitioned"></a>[`server.servlet.session.cookie.partitioned`](#application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.cookie.partitioned) | Whether the generated cookie carries the Partitioned attribute. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.store-dir"></a>[`server.servlet.session.store-dir`](#application-properties.server.server.servlet.session.store-dir) | Directory used to store session data. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.ssl.trust-store-type"></a>[`server.ssl.trust-store-type`](#application-properties.server.server.ssl.trust-store-type) | Type of the trust store. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.buffered"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.buffered`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.buffered) | Whether to buffer output such that it is flushed only periodically. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.check-exists"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.check-exists`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.check-exists) | Whether to check for log file existence so it can be recreated if an external process has renamed it. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.condition-if"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.condition-if`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.condition-if) | Whether logging of the request will only be enabled if "ServletRequest.getAttribute(conditionIf)" does not yield null. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.condition-unless"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.condition-unless`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.condition-unless) | Whether logging of the request will only be enabled if "ServletRequest.getAttribute(conditionUnless)" yield null. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.enabled"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.enabled`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.enabled) | Enable access log. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.ipv6-canonical"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.ipv6-canonical`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.ipv6-canonical) | Whether to use IPv6 canonical representation format as defined by RFC 5952. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.max-days"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.max-days`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.max-days) | Number of days to retain the access log files before they are removed. | `-1` |
