| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.prefix"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.prefix`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.prefix) | Log file name prefix. | `access_log` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.rename-on-rotate"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.rename-on-rotate`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.rename-on-rotate) | Whether to defer inclusion of the date stamp in the file name until rotate time. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.request-attributes-enabled"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.request-attributes-enabled`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.request-attributes-enabled) | Set request attributes for the IP address, Hostname, protocol, and port used for the request. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.rotate"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.rotate`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.rotate) | Whether to enable access log rotation. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.suffix"></a>[`server.tomcat.accesslog.suffix`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.accesslog.suffix) | Log file name suffix. | `.log` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.additional-tld-skip-patterns"></a>[`server.tomcat.additional-tld-skip-patterns`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.additional-tld-skip-patterns) | List of additional patterns that match jars to ignore for TLD scanning. The special '?' and '\*' characters can be used in the pattern to match one and only one character and zero or more characters respectively. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.background-processor-delay"></a>[`server.tomcat.background-processor-delay`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.background-processor-delay) | Delay between the invocation of backgroundProcess methods. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `10s` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.basedir"></a>[`server.tomcat.basedir`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.basedir) | Tomcat base directory. If not specified, a temporary directory is used. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.connection-timeout"></a>[`server.tomcat.connection-timeout`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.connection-timeout) | Amount of time the connector will wait, after accepting a connection, for the request URI line to be presented. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.keep-alive-timeout"></a>[`server.tomcat.keep-alive-timeout`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.keep-alive-timeout) | Time to wait for another HTTP request before the connection is closed. When not set the connectionTimeout is used. When set to -1 there will be no timeout. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-connections"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-connections`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-connections) | Maximum number of connections that the server accepts and processes at any given time. Once the limit has been reached, the operating system may still accept connections based on the "acceptCount" property. | `8192` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-http-form-post-size"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-http-form-post-size`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-http-form-post-size) | Maximum size of the form content in any HTTP post request. | `2MB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-http-response-header-size"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-http-response-header-size`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-http-response-header-size) | Maximum size of the HTTP response header. | `8KB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-keep-alive-requests"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-keep-alive-requests`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-keep-alive-requests) | Maximum number of HTTP requests that can be pipelined before the connection is closed. When set to 0 or 1, keep-alive and pipelining are disabled. When set to -1, an unlimited number of pipelined or keep-alive requests are allowed. | `100` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-parameter-count"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-parameter-count`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-parameter-count) | Maximum number of parameters (GET plus POST) that will be automatically parsed by the container. A value of less than 0 means no limit. | `1000` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-part-count"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-part-count`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-part-count) | Maximum total number of parts permitted in a multipart/form-data request. Requests that exceed this limit will be rejected. A value of less than 0 means no limit. | `50` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-part-header-size"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-part-header-size`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-part-header-size) | Maximum per-part header size permitted in a multipart/form-data request. Requests that exceed this limit will be rejected. A value of less than 0 means no limit. | `512B` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-swallow-size"></a>[`server.tomcat.max-swallow-size`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.max-swallow-size) | Maximum amount of request body to swallow. | `2MB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.mbeanregistry.enabled"></a>[`server.tomcat.mbeanregistry.enabled`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.mbeanregistry.enabled) | Whether Tomcat's MBean Registry should be enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.processor-cache"></a>[`server.tomcat.processor-cache`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.processor-cache) | Maximum number of idle processors that will be retained in the cache and reused with a subsequent request. When set to -1 the cache will be unlimited with a theoretical maximum size equal to the maximum number of connections. | `200` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.redirect-context-root"></a>[`server.tomcat.redirect-context-root`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.redirect-context-root) | Whether requests to the context root should be redirected by appending a / to the path. When using SSL terminated at a proxy, this property should be set to false. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.relaxed-path-chars"></a>[`server.tomcat.relaxed-path-chars`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.relaxed-path-chars) | List of additional unencoded characters that should be allowed in URI paths. Only "< > \[ \\ \] ^ ` { \| }" are allowed. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.relaxed-query-chars"></a>[`server.tomcat.relaxed-query-chars`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.relaxed-query-chars) | List of additional unencoded characters that should be allowed in URI query strings. Only "< > \[ \\ \] ^ ` { \| }" are allowed. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.host-header"></a>[`server.tomcat.remoteip.host-header`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.host-header) | Name of the HTTP header from which the remote host is extracted. | `X-Forwarded-Host` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.internal-proxies"></a>[`server.tomcat.remoteip.internal-proxies`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.internal-proxies) | Internal proxies that are to be trusted. Can be set as a comma separate list of CIDR or as a regular expression. | `192.168.0.0/16, 172.16.0.0/12, 169.254.0.0/16, fc00::/7, 10.0.0.0/8, 100.64.0.0/10, 127.0.0.0/8, fe80::/10, ::1/128` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.port-header"></a>[`server.tomcat.remoteip.port-header`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.port-header) | Name of the HTTP header used to override the original port value. | `X-Forwarded-Port` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.protocol-header"></a>[`server.tomcat.remoteip.protocol-header`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.protocol-header) | Header that holds the incoming protocol, usually named "X-Forwarded-Proto". |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.protocol-header-https-value"></a>[`server.tomcat.remoteip.protocol-header-https-value`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.protocol-header-https-value) | Value of the protocol header indicating whether the incoming request uses SSL. | `https` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.remote-ip-header"></a>[`server.tomcat.remoteip.remote-ip-header`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.remote-ip-header) | Name of the HTTP header from which the remote IP is extracted. For instance, 'X-FORWARDED-FOR'. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.trusted-proxies"></a>[`server.tomcat.remoteip.trusted-proxies`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.remoteip.trusted-proxies) | Regular expression defining proxies that are trusted when they appear in the "remote-ip-header" header. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.resource.allow-caching"></a>[`server.tomcat.resource.allow-caching`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.resource.allow-caching) | Whether static resource caching is permitted for this web application. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.resource.cache-max-size"></a>[`server.tomcat.resource.cache-max-size`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.resource.cache-max-size) | Maximum size of the static resource cache. | `10MB` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.resource.cache-ttl"></a>[`server.tomcat.resource.cache-ttl`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.resource.cache-ttl) | Time-to-live of the static resource cache. | `5s` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.threads.max"></a>[`server.tomcat.threads.max`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.threads.max) | Maximum amount of worker threads. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. | `200` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.threads.max-queue-capacity"></a>[`server.tomcat.threads.max-queue-capacity`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.threads.max-queue-capacity) | Maximum capacity of the thread pool's backing queue. This setting only has an effect if the value is greater than 0. | `2147483647` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.threads.min-spare"></a>[`server.tomcat.threads.min-spare`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.threads.min-spare) | Minimum amount of worker threads. Doesn't have an effect if virtual threads are enabled. | `10` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.uri-encoding"></a>[`server.tomcat.uri-encoding`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.uri-encoding) | Character encoding to use to decode the URI. | `UTF-8` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.use-apr"></a>[`server.tomcat.use-apr`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.use-apr) | Whether to use APR. | `never` |
| <a id="application-properties.server.server.tomcat.use-relative-redirects"></a>[`server.tomcat.use-relative-redirects`](#application-properties.server.server.tomcat.use-relative-redirects) | Whether HTTP 1.1 and later location headers generated by a call to sendRedirect will use relative or absolute redirects. | `false` |

<a id="appendix.application-properties.templating"></a>

## Templating Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.allow-request-override"></a>[`spring.freemarker.allow-request-override`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.allow-request-override) | Whether HttpServletRequest attributes are allowed to override (hide) controller generated model attributes of the same name. Only supported with Spring MVC. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.allow-session-override"></a>[`spring.freemarker.allow-session-override`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.allow-session-override) | Whether HttpSession attributes are allowed to override (hide) controller generated model attributes of the same name. Only supported with Spring MVC. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.cache"></a>[`spring.freemarker.cache`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.cache) | Whether to enable template caching. Only supported with Spring MVC. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.charset"></a>[`spring.freemarker.charset`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.charset) | Template encoding. | `UTF-8` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.check-template-location"></a>[`spring.freemarker.check-template-location`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.check-template-location) | Whether to check that the templates location exists. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.content-type"></a>[`spring.freemarker.content-type`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.content-type) | Content-Type value. Only supported with Spring MVC. | `text/html` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.expose-request-attributes"></a>[`spring.freemarker.expose-request-attributes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.expose-request-attributes) | Whether all request attributes should be added to the model prior to merging with the template. Only supported with Spring MVC. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.expose-session-attributes"></a>[`spring.freemarker.expose-session-attributes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.expose-session-attributes) | Whether all HttpSession attributes should be added to the model prior to merging with the template. Only supported with Spring MVC. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.expose-spring-macro-helpers"></a>[`spring.freemarker.expose-spring-macro-helpers`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.expose-spring-macro-helpers) | Whether to expose a RequestContext for use by Spring's macro library, under the name "springMacroRequestContext". Only supported with Spring MVC. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.prefer-file-system-access"></a>[`spring.freemarker.prefer-file-system-access`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.prefer-file-system-access) | Whether to prefer file system access for template loading to enable hot detection of template changes. When a template path is detected as a directory, templates are loaded from the directory only and other matching classpath locations will not be considered. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.prefix"></a>[`spring.freemarker.prefix`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.prefix) | Prefix that gets prepended to view names when building a URL. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.request-context-attribute"></a>[`spring.freemarker.request-context-attribute`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.request-context-attribute) | Name of the RequestContext attribute for all views. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.settings"></a>[`spring.freemarker.settings.*`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.settings) | Well-known FreeMarker keys which are passed to FreeMarker's Configuration. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.suffix"></a>[`spring.freemarker.suffix`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.suffix) | Suffix that gets appended to view names when building a URL. | `.ftlh` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.template-loader-path"></a>[`spring.freemarker.template-loader-path`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.template-loader-path) | List of template paths. | `[classpath:/templates/]` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.view-names"></a>[`spring.freemarker.view-names`](#application-properties.templating.spring.freemarker.view-names) | View names that can be resolved. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.allow-request-override"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.allow-request-override`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.allow-request-override) | Whether HttpServletRequest attributes are allowed to override (hide) controller generated model attributes of the same name. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.allow-session-override"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.allow-session-override`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.allow-session-override) | Whether HttpSession attributes are allowed to override (hide) controller generated model attributes of the same name. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-escape"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.auto-escape`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-escape) | Whether models that are assignable to CharSequence are escaped automatically. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-indent"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.auto-indent`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-indent) | Whether indents are rendered automatically. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-indent-string"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.auto-indent-string`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-indent-string) | String used for auto-indents. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-new-line"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.auto-new-line`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.auto-new-line) | Whether new lines are rendered automatically. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.base-template-class"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.base-template-class`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.base-template-class) | Template base class. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.cache"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.cache`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.cache) | Whether to enable template caching. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.charset"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.charset`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.charset) | Template encoding. | `UTF-8` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.check-template-location"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.check-template-location`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.check-template-location) | Whether to check that the templates location exists. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.content-type"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.content-type`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.content-type) | Content-Type value. | `text/html` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.declaration-encoding"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.declaration-encoding`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.declaration-encoding) | Encoding used to write the declaration heading. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expand-empty-elements"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.expand-empty-elements`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expand-empty-elements) | Whether elements without a body should be written expanded (<br></br>) or not (<br/>). | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expose-request-attributes"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.expose-request-attributes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expose-request-attributes) | Whether all request attributes should be added to the model prior to merging with the template. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expose-session-attributes"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.expose-session-attributes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expose-session-attributes) | Whether all HttpSession attributes should be added to the model prior to merging with the template. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expose-spring-macro-helpers"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.expose-spring-macro-helpers`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.expose-spring-macro-helpers) | Whether to expose a RequestContext for use by Spring's macro library, under the name "springMacroRequestContext". | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.locale"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.locale`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.locale) | Default locale for template resolution. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.new-line-string"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.new-line-string`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.new-line-string) | String used to write a new line. Defaults to the system's line separator. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.prefix"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.prefix`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.prefix) | Prefix that gets prepended to view names when building a URL. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.request-context-attribute"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.request-context-attribute`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.request-context-attribute) | Name of the RequestContext attribute for all views. | `spring` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.resource-loader-path"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.resource-loader-path`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.resource-loader-path) | Template path. | `classpath:/templates/` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.suffix"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.suffix`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.suffix) | Suffix that gets appended to view names when building a URL. | `.tpl` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.use-double-quotes"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.use-double-quotes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.use-double-quotes) | Whether attributes should use double quotes. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.view-names"></a>[`spring.groovy.template.view-names`](#application-properties.templating.spring.groovy.template.view-names) | View names that can be resolved. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.prefix"></a>[`spring.mustache.prefix`](#application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.prefix) | Prefix to apply to template names. | `classpath:/templates/` |

| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.allow-request-override"></a>[`spring.mustache.servlet.allow-request-override`](#application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.allow-request-override) | Whether HttpServletRequest attributes are allowed to override (hide) controller generated model attributes of the same name. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.allow-session-override"></a>[`spring.mustache.servlet.allow-session-override`](#application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.allow-session-override) | Whether HttpSession attributes are allowed to override (hide) controller generated model attributes of the same name. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.cache"></a>[`spring.mustache.servlet.cache`](#application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.cache) | Whether to enable template caching. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.content-type"></a>[`spring.mustache.servlet.content-type`](#application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.content-type) | Content-Type value. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.expose-request-attributes"></a>[`spring.mustache.servlet.expose-request-attributes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.expose-request-attributes) | Whether all request attributes should be added to the model prior to merging with the template. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.expose-session-attributes"></a>[`spring.mustache.servlet.expose-session-attributes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.mustache.servlet.expose-session-attributes) | Whether all HttpSession attributes should be added to the model prior to merging with the template. | `false` |

| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.cache"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.cache`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.cache) | Whether to enable template caching. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.check-template"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.check-template`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.check-template) | Whether to check that the template exists before rendering it. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.check-template-location"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.check-template-location`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.check-template-location) | Whether to check that the templates location exists. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.enable-spring-el-compiler"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.enable-spring-el-compiler`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.enable-spring-el-compiler) | Enable the SpringEL compiler in SpringEL expressions. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.encoding"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.encoding`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.encoding) | Template files encoding. | `UTF-8` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.excluded-view-names"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.excluded-view-names`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.excluded-view-names) | List of view names (patterns allowed) that should be excluded from resolution. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.mode"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.mode`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.mode) | Template mode to be applied to templates. See also Thymeleaf's TemplateMode enum. | `HTML` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.prefix"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.prefix`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.prefix) | Prefix that gets prepended to view names when building a URL. | `classpath:/templates/` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.chunked-mode-view-names"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.reactive.chunked-mode-view-names`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.chunked-mode-view-names) | Comma-separated list of view names (patterns allowed) that should be the only ones executed in CHUNKED mode when a max chunk size is set. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.full-mode-view-names"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.reactive.full-mode-view-names`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.full-mode-view-names) | Comma-separated list of view names (patterns allowed) that should be executed in FULL mode even if a max chunk size is set. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.max-chunk-size"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.reactive.max-chunk-size`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.max-chunk-size) | Maximum size of data buffers used for writing to the response. Templates will execute in CHUNKED mode by default if this is set. | `0B` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.media-types"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.reactive.media-types`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.reactive.media-types) | Media types supported by the view technology. | `[text/html, application/xhtml+xml, application/xml, text/xml, application/rss+xml, application/atom+xml, application/javascript, application/ecmascript, text/javascript, text/ecmascript, application/json, text/css, text/plain, text/event-stream]` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.render-hidden-markers-before-checkboxes"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.render-hidden-markers-before-checkboxes`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.render-hidden-markers-before-checkboxes) | Whether hidden form inputs acting as markers for checkboxes should be rendered before the checkbox element itself. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.servlet.content-type"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.servlet.content-type`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.servlet.content-type) | Content-Type value written to HTTP responses. | `text/html` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.servlet.produce-partial-output-while-processing"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.servlet.produce-partial-output-while-processing`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.servlet.produce-partial-output-while-processing) | Whether Thymeleaf should start writing partial output as soon as possible or buffer until template processing is finished. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.suffix"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.suffix`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.suffix) | Suffix that gets appended to view names when building a URL. | `.html` |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.template-resolver-order"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.template-resolver-order`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.template-resolver-order) | Order of the template resolver in the chain. By default, the template resolver is first in the chain. Order start at 1 and should only be set if you have defined additional "TemplateResolver" beans. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.view-names"></a>[`spring.thymeleaf.view-names`](#application-properties.templating.spring.thymeleaf.view-names) | List of view names (patterns allowed) that can be resolved. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.testing.spring.test.tracing.export"></a>[`spring.test.tracing.export`](#application-properties.testing.spring.test.tracing.export) | Whether tracing export should be auto-configured in tests. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.testing.spring.test.webservice.client.mockserver.enabled"></a>[`spring.test.webservice.client.mockserver.enabled`](#application-properties.testing.spring.test.webservice.client.mockserver.enabled) | Whether a MockWebServiceServer should be auto-configured. | `true` |

## Web Properties

| Name | Description | Default Value |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allow-credentials"></a>[`spring.graphql.cors.allow-credentials`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allow-credentials) | Whether credentials are supported. When not set, credentials are not supported. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-headers"></a>[`spring.graphql.cors.allowed-headers`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-headers) | List of HTTP headers to allow in a request. '\*' allows all headers. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-methods"></a>[`spring.graphql.cors.allowed-methods`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-methods) | List of HTTP methods to allow. '\*' allows all methods. When not set, defaults to GET. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-origin-patterns"></a>[`spring.graphql.cors.allowed-origin-patterns`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-origin-patterns) | List of origin patterns to allow. Unlike allowed origins which only support '\*', origin patterns are more flexible, e.g. 'https://\*.example.com', and can be used with allow-credentials. When neither allowed origins nor allowed origin patterns are set, cross-origin requests are effectively disabled. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-origins"></a>[`spring.graphql.cors.allowed-origins`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.allowed-origins) | List of origins to allow with '\*' allowing all origins. When allow-credentials is enabled, '\*' cannot be used, and setting origin patterns should be considered instead. When neither allowed origins nor allowed origin patterns are set, cross-origin requests are effectively disabled. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.exposed-headers"></a>[`spring.graphql.cors.exposed-headers`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.exposed-headers) | List of headers to include in a response. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.max-age"></a>[`spring.graphql.cors.max-age`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.cors.max-age) | How long the response from a pre-flight request can be cached by clients. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. | `1800s` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.graphiql.enabled"></a>[`spring.graphql.graphiql.enabled`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.graphiql.enabled) | Whether the default GraphiQL UI is enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.graphiql.path"></a>[`spring.graphql.graphiql.path`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.graphiql.path) | Path to the GraphiQL UI endpoint. | `/graphiql` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.http.path"></a>[`spring.graphql.http.path`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.http.path) | Path at which to expose a GraphQL request HTTP endpoint. | `/graphql` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.http.sse.keep-alive"></a>[`spring.graphql.http.sse.keep-alive`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.http.sse.keep-alive) | How frequently keep-alive messages should be sent. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.http.sse.timeout"></a>[`spring.graphql.http.sse.timeout`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.http.sse.timeout) | Time required for concurrent handling to complete. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.rsocket.mapping"></a>[`spring.graphql.rsocket.mapping`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.rsocket.mapping) | Mapping of the RSocket message handler. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.rsocket.preferred-json-mapper"></a>[`spring.graphql.rsocket.preferred-json-mapper`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.rsocket.preferred-json-mapper) | Preferred JSON mapper to use. By default, auto-detected according to the environment. Supported values are 'jackson' and 'jackson2' (deprecated). | `jackson` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.additional-files"></a>[`spring.graphql.schema.additional-files`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.additional-files) | Locations of additional, individual schema files to parse. | `[]` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.file-extensions"></a>[`spring.graphql.schema.file-extensions`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.file-extensions) | File extensions for GraphQL schema files. | `.graphqls,.gqls` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.inspection.enabled"></a>[`spring.graphql.schema.inspection.enabled`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.inspection.enabled) | Whether schema should be compared to the application to detect missing mappings. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.introspection.enabled"></a>[`spring.graphql.schema.introspection.enabled`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.introspection.enabled) | Whether field introspection should be enabled at the schema level. | `true` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.locations"></a>[`spring.graphql.schema.locations`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.locations) | Locations of GraphQL schema files. | `classpath:graphql/**/` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.printer.enabled"></a>[`spring.graphql.schema.printer.enabled`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.schema.printer.enabled) | Whether the endpoint that prints the schema is enabled. Schema is available under spring.graphql.http.path + "/schema". | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.websocket.connection-init-timeout"></a>[`spring.graphql.websocket.connection-init-timeout`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.websocket.connection-init-timeout) | Time within which the initial CONNECTION\_INIT type message must be received. | `60s` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.websocket.keep-alive"></a>[`spring.graphql.websocket.keep-alive`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.websocket.keep-alive) | Maximum idle period before a server keep-alive ping is sent to client. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.graphql.websocket.path"></a>[`spring.graphql.websocket.path`](#application-properties.web.spring.graphql.websocket.path) | Path of the GraphQL WebSocket subscription endpoint. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.http.clients.read-timeout"></a>[`spring.http.clients.read-timeout`](#application-properties.web.spring.http.clients.read-timeout) | Default read timeout for a client HTTP request. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.http.codecs.max-in-memory-size"></a>[`spring.http.codecs.max-in-memory-size`](#application-properties.web.spring.http.codecs.max-in-memory-size) | Limit on the number of bytes that can be buffered whenever the input stream needs to be aggregated. This applies only to the auto-configured WebFlux server and WebClient instances. By default this is not set, in which case individual codec defaults apply. Most codecs are limited to 256K by default. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.http.codecs.preferred-json-mapper"></a>[`spring.http.codecs.preferred-json-mapper`](#application-properties.web.spring.http.codecs.preferred-json-mapper) | Preferred JSON mapper to use for HTTP encoding and decoding. By default, auto-detected according to the environment. Supported values are 'jackson' and 'jackson2' (deprecated). | `jackson` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.http.converters.preferred-json-mapper"></a>[`spring.http.converters.preferred-json-mapper`](#application-properties.web.spring.http.converters.preferred-json-mapper) | Preferred JSON mapper to use for HTTP message conversion. By default, auto-detected according to the environment. Supported values are 'jackson', 'jackson2' (deprecated), 'gson' and 'jsonb'. | <a id="application-properties.web.spring.mvc.dispatch-options-request"></a>[`spring.mvc.dispatch-options-request`](#application-properties.web.spring.mvc.dispatch-options-request) | Whether to dispatch OPTIONS requests to the FrameworkServlet doService method. | `true` |

| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.mvc.format.date-time"></a>[`spring.mvc.format.date-time`](#application-properties.web.spring.mvc.format.date-time) | Date-time format to use, for example 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'. | <a id="application-properties.web.spring.mvc.format.time"></a>[`spring.mvc.format.time`](#application-properties.web.spring.mvc.format.time) | Time format to use, for example 'HH:mm:ss'. Used for formatting of java.time's LocalTime and OffsetTime. |  |

| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.cache-public"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.cache-public`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.cache-public) | Indicate that any cache may store the response. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.max-age"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.max-age`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.max-age) | Maximum time the response should be cached, in seconds if no duration suffix is not specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.must-revalidate"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.must-revalidate`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.must-revalidate) | Indicate that once it has become stale, a cache must not use the response without re-validating it with the server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-cache"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-cache`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-cache) | Indicate that the cached response can be reused only if re-validated with the server. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-store"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-store`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-store) | Indicate to not cache the response in any case. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-transform"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-transform`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.no-transform) | Indicate intermediaries (caches and others) that they should not transform the response content. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.proxy-revalidate"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.proxy-revalidate`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.proxy-revalidate) | Same meaning as the "must-revalidate" directive, except that it does not apply to private caches. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.s-max-age"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.s-max-age`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.s-max-age) | Maximum time the response should be cached by shared caches, in seconds if no duration suffix is not specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.stale-if-error"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.stale-if-error`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.stale-if-error) | Maximum time the response may be used when errors are encountered, in seconds if no duration suffix is not specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.stale-while-revalidate"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.stale-while-revalidate`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol.stale-while-revalidate) | Maximum time the response can be served after it becomes stale, in seconds if no duration suffix is not specified. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.period"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.period`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.period) | Cache period for the resources served by the resource handler. If a duration suffix is not specified, seconds will be used. Can be overridden by the 'spring.web.resources.cache.cachecontrol' properties. |  |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.use-last-modified"></a>[`spring.web.resources.cache.use-last-modified`](#application-properties.web.spring.web.resources.cache.use-last-modified) | Whether we should use the "lastModified" metadata of the files in HTTP caching headers. | `true` |

| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.webflux.multipart.max-in-memory-size"></a>[`spring.webflux.multipart.max-in-memory-size`](#application-properties.web.spring.webflux.multipart.max-in-memory-size) | Maximum amount of memory allowed per part before it's written to disk. Set to -1 to store all contents in memory. | `256KB` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.webflux.multipart.max-parts"></a>[`spring.webflux.multipart.max-parts`](#application-properties.web.spring.webflux.multipart.max-parts) | Maximum number of parts allowed in a given multipart request. Default is -1 which enforces no limits. | `-1` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.webflux.problemdetails.enabled"></a>[`spring.webflux.problemdetails.enabled`](#application-properties.web.spring.webflux.problemdetails.enabled) | Whether RFC 9457 Problem Details support should be enabled. | `false` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.webflux.static-path-pattern"></a>[`spring.webflux.static-path-pattern`](#application-properties.web.spring.webflux.static-path-pattern) | Path pattern used for static resources. | `/**` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.webflux.webjars-path-pattern"></a>[`spring.webflux.webjars-path-pattern`](#application-properties.web.spring.webflux.webjars-path-pattern) | Path pattern used for WebJar assets. | `/webjars/**` |
| <a id="application-properties.web.spring.websocket.messaging.preferred-json-mapper"></a>[`spring.websocket.messaging.preferred-json-mapper`](#application-properties.web.spring.websocket.messaging.preferred-json-mapper) | Preferred JSON mapper to use for WebSocket messaging. By default, auto-detected according to the environment. Supported values are 'jackson' and 'jackson2' (deprecated). | `jackson` |
